

// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\App.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — App Principal
// Roteamento com proteção por autenticação e role
// ============================================================

import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "./context/AuthContext";
import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import LoginPage from "./pages/LoginPage";
import CompaniesTab from "./pages/CompaniesTab";
import PermissionsTab from "./pages/PermissionsTab";
import DashboardTab from "./pages/DashboardTab";
import DevicesTab from "./pages/DevicesTab";
import CampaignsTab from "./pages/CampaignsTab";
import AutomationsTab from "./pages/AutomationsTab";
import LogsTab from "./pages/LogsTab";
import SettingsTab from "./pages/SettingsTab";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

// ---- Protected Route ----
function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { loggedIn } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  if (!loggedIn) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" state={{ from: location }} replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

// ---- Role Gate ----
function RoleGate({ roles, children }: { roles: string[]; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  if (!user || !roles.includes(user.role)) {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

// ---- Company Gate (requer empresa selecionada) ----
function CompanyGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { user, selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();

  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;

  // Master sem empresa selecionada → vai para companies
  if (user.role === "master" && selectedCompanyId === null) {
    return <Navigate to="/companies" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

// ---- Root Redirect ----
function RootRedirect() {
  const { user, selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();

  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;

  // Master sem empresa → Companies
  if (user.role === "master" && selectedCompanyId === null) {
    return <Navigate to="/companies" replace />;
  }

  // Com empresa selecionada → Devices
  return <Navigate to="/devices" replace />;
}

// ---- App ----
function AppRoutes() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      {/* Login */}
      <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />

      {/* Protected Routes */}
      <Route
        element={
          <ProtectedRoute>
            <Layout />
          </ProtectedRoute>
        }
      >
        {/* Root Redirect */}
        <Route index element={<RootRedirect />} />

        {/* Companies (Master only) */}
        <Route
          path="/companies"
          element={
            <RoleGate roles={["master"]}>
              <CompaniesTab />
            </RoleGate>
          }
        />

        {/* Permissions (Master only) */}
        <Route
          path="/permissions"
          element={
            <RoleGate roles={["master"]}>
              <PermissionsTab />
            </RoleGate>
          }
        />

        {/* Dashboard (requer empresa) */}
        <Route
          path="/dashboard"
          element={
            <CompanyGate>
              <DashboardTab />
            </CompanyGate>
          }
        />

        {/* Tabs que requerem empresa */}
        <Route
          path="/devices"
          element={
            <CompanyGate>
              <DevicesTab />
            </CompanyGate>
          }
        />
        <Route
          path="/campaigns"
          element={
            <CompanyGate>
              <RoleGate roles={["master", "gestor"]}>
                <CampaignsTab />
              </RoleGate>
            </CompanyGate>
          }
        />
        <Route
          path="/automations"
          element={
            <CompanyGate>
              <RoleGate roles={["master", "gestor"]}>
                <AutomationsTab />
              </RoleGate>
            </CompanyGate>
          }
        />
        <Route
          path="/logs"
          element={
            <CompanyGate>
              <LogsTab />
            </CompanyGate>
          }
        />
        <Route
          path="/settings"
          element={
            <CompanyGate>
              <RoleGate roles={["master", "gestor"]}>
                <SettingsTab />
              </RoleGate>
            </CompanyGate>
          }
        />
      </Route>

      {/* Catch all */}
      <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AppRoutes />
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
}

export type Role = "master" | "gestor" | "colaborador";

export interface User {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  displayName: string;
  role: Role;
  companyId: number | null;
  avatar?: string;
}

export interface Company {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  deviceLimit: number;
  userLimit: number;
  deviceCount: number;
  userCount: number;
  status: "active" | "inactive";
  createdAt: string;
}

export type DeviceStatus = "online" | "offline" | "connecting" | "error";

export interface Device {
  id: number;
  serial: string;
  name?: string;
  model: string;
  brand: string;
  status: DeviceStatus;
  battery: number;
  companyId: number;
  ip?: string;
  port?: number;
  lastSeen?: string;
  deviceNumber: number;
  sector: string;
}

export type CampaignStatus = "running" | "paused" | "completed" | "draft" | "error";

export interface Campaign {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  greeting: string;
  message: string;
  closing: string;
  delayMin: number;
  delayMax: number;
  status: CampaignStatus;
  contactsCount: number;
  sentCount: number;
  failedCount: number;
  deviceIds?: number[];
  companyId: number;
  useSpintax: boolean;
  useAI: boolean;
  aiModel?: string;
  aiTemperature?: number;
  aiPrompt?: string;
  createdAt?: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface Automation {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  keyword: string;
  response: string;
  active: boolean;
  companyId: number;
  triggeredCount: number;
  createdAt: string;
  // Extended trigger/action fields
  triggerType: AutomationTriggerType;
  actionType: AutomationActionType;
  triggerConfig: string; // JSON config for trigger
  actionConfig: string;  // JSON config for action
  sector?: string;
}

export type AutomationTriggerType =
  | "keyword"
  | "ban_detected"
  | "device_offline"
  | "schedule"
  | "message_received";

export type AutomationActionType =
  | "send_response"
  | "move_chip"
  | "notify"
  | "toggle_campaign"
  | "set_chip_status"
  | "send_to_sector";

export const TRIGGER_LABELS: Record<AutomationTriggerType, string> = {
  keyword: "Palavra-chave",
  ban_detected: "Ban Detectado",
  device_offline: "Dispositivo Offline",
  schedule: "Agendamento",
  message_received: "Mensagem Recebida",
};

export const ACTION_LABELS: Record<AutomationActionType, string> = {
  send_response: "Enviar Resposta",
  move_chip: "Mover Chip",
  notify: "Notificar Gestor",
  toggle_campaign: "Pausar/Iniciar Campanha",
  set_chip_status: "Alterar Status do Chip",
  send_to_sector: "Encaminhar para Setor",
};

export type ChipStatus =
  | "livre"
  | "em_uso"
  | "banido"
  | "restrito_24h"
  | "restrito_7d"
  | "restrito_custom";

  id: number;
  deviceId: number;
  phoneNumber: string;
  chipName: string;
  status: ChipStatus;
  holder?: string;
  isReserve?: boolean;
  linkedChipId?: number;
  restrictedUntil?: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export type ContactStatus = "pending" | "sent" | "failed" | "replied" | "processing";

export interface Contact {
  id: number;
  contactName?: string;
  name?: string;
  phone: string;
  status: ContactStatus;
  campaignId: number;

  sentAt?: string;
}

}

}

}

export interface DevicePrefs {

export interface AiConfig {

}

export interface Permission {

}

}

}

} from "lucide-react";

const PAGE_TITLES: Record<string, { title: string; subtitle: string }> = {
  "/companies": { title: "Gestão de Empresas", subtitle: "Gerencie permissões e cotas de clientes" },
  "/devices": { title: "Hub de Dispositivos ADB", subtitle: "Conexões ativas e status em tempo real" },
  "/campaigns": { title: "Central de Campanhas", subtitle: "Disparos inteligentes e fluxo de contatos" },
  "/automations": { title: "Regras de Automação", subtitle: "Respostas automáticas por palavras-chave" },
  "/logs": { title: "Logs do Sistema", subtitle: "Histórico detalhado de eventos e execuções" },
  "/settings": { title: "Configurações da Empresa", subtitle: "Preferências e conexões de infraestrutura" },
};

export default function Header() {
  const { user, selectedCompanyId, enterCompany, leaveCompany, logout } = useAuth();
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [companyDropdownOpen, setCompanyDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const [userMenuOpen, setUserMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  if (!user) return null;

  const currentRoute = PAGE_TITLES[location.pathname] || {
    title: "Esfera v3",
    subtitle: "Plataforma de Disparos Inteligentes",
  };

  const selectedCompany = MOCK_COMPANIES.find((c) => c.id === selectedCompanyId);
  const companyDevices = MOCK_DEVICES.filter((d) => d.companyId === selectedCompanyId);
  const onlineDevicesCount = companyDevices.filter((d) => d.status === "online").length;

  const handleCompanySelect = (companyId: number) => {
    enterCompany(companyId);
    setCompanyDropdownOpen(false);
    navigate("/devices");
  };

  const handleLeaveCompany = () => {
    leaveCompany();
    setCompanyDropdownOpen(false);
    navigate("/companies");
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 h-16 w-full glass-header flex items-center justify-between px-6 transition-all">
      {/* Left: Title & Context */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">
              {currentRoute.title}
            </h1>
            {selectedCompany && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium bg-violet-500/10 text-violet-400 border border-violet-500/20">
                <Building2 size={11} />
                {selectedCompany.name}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-tertiary hidden sm:block">{currentRoute.subtitle}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right: Actions, Search, Switcher & User */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {/* Quick Search Bar */}
        <div className="relative hidden md:flex items-center">
          <Search size={15} className="absolute left-3.5 text-tertiary pointer-events-none z-10" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Pesquisa rápida (Ctrl+K)..."
            className="w-56 input-with-icon-left focus:w-64 focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Engine ADB Status Badge */}
        <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-border text-xs">
          <Cpu size={14} className="text-indigo-400" />
          <span className="text-tertiary">ADB Engine:</span>
          <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
            {onlineDevicesCount > 0 ? `${onlineDevicesCount} Online` : "Pronto"}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Company Switcher Dropdown (Master only) */}
        {user.role === "master" && (
          <div className="relative">
            <button
              onClick={() => setCompanyDropdownOpen(!companyDropdownOpen)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border hover:bg-white/10 text-xs font-medium text-primary transition-all cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Building2 size={14} className="text-violet-400" />
              <span className="max-w-[120px] truncate">
                {selectedCompany ? selectedCompany.name : "Selecionar Empresa"}
              </span>
              <ChevronDown size={14} className="text-tertiary" />
            </button>

            {companyDropdownOpen && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-56 rounded-2xl glass-panel shadow-2xl p-1.5 z-50 animate-scale-in">
                <div className="px-3 py-2 text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-tertiary tracking-wider border-b border-border mb-1">
                  Trocar Empresa
                </div>
                {MOCK_COMPANIES.map((c) => (
                  <button
                    key={c.id}
                    onClick={() => handleCompanySelect(c.id)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                      selectedCompanyId === c.id
                        ? "bg-violet-600/20 text-violet-300 font-semibold"
                        : "text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/5"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="truncate">{c.name}</span>
                    {selectedCompanyId === c.id && <CheckCircle2 size={13} className="text-violet-400" />}
                  </button>
                ))}
                {selectedCompanyId !== null && (
                  <button
                    onClick={handleLeaveCompany}
                    className="w-full text-left px-3 py-2 mt-1 border-t border-border text-xs text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/10 rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Ver todas as empresas
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Theme Toggle */}
        <button
          onClick={toggleTheme}
          className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/10 transition-all cursor-pointer"
          title={isDark ? "Modo Claro" : "Modo Escuro"}
        >
          {isDark ? <Sun size={16} /> : <Moon size={16} />}
        </button>

        {/* User Profile Menu */}
        <div className="relative">
          <button
            onClick={() => setUserMenuOpen(!userMenuOpen)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 p-1 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/5 transition-all cursor-pointer"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md shadow-violet-500/20">
              {user.displayName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </div>
          </button>

          {userMenuOpen && (
            <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-48 rounded-2xl glass-panel shadow-2xl p-2 z-50 animate-scale-in">
              <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-border mb-1">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary truncate">{user.displayName}</p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-tertiary capitalize">{user.role}</p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setUserMenuOpen(false);
                  navigate("/settings");
                }}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <UserCheck size={14} />
                <span>Perfil & Ajustes</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setUserMenuOpen(false);
                  logout();
                  navigate("/login");
                }}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/10 transition-colors cursor-pointer mt-1"
              >
                <LogOut size={14} />
                <span>Sair da conta</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\Sidebar.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Sidebar Lateral com Permissões Granulares
// Filtra itens de navegação por role E permissões do usuário
// ============================================================

import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { usePermissions } from "../hooks/usePermissions";
import { MOCK_COMPANIES } from "../mock-data";
import {
  Smartphone,
  Megaphone,
  Bot,
  ScrollText,
  Settings,
  Building2,
  LogOut,
  ChevronLeft,
  Zap,
  Shield,
  BarChart3,
} from "lucide-react";

interface NavGroup {
  groupName: string;
  items: {
    id: string;
    path: string;
    label: string;
    icon: React.ReactNode;
    roles: string[];
    requiresCompany: boolean;
    permissionKey?: string;
    badge?: string;
  }[];
}

const NAV_GROUPS: NavGroup[] = [
  {
    groupName: "GERENCIAMENTO",
    items: [
      {
        id: "companies",
        path: "/companies",
        label: "Empresas",
        icon: <Building2 size={18} />,
        roles: ["master"],
        requiresCompany: false,
      },
      {
        id: "permissions",
        path: "/permissions",
        label: "Permissões",
        icon: <Shield size={18} />,
        roles: ["master"],
        requiresCompany: false,
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    groupName: "OPERAÇÕES",
    items: [
      {
        id: "dashboard",
        path: "/dashboard",
        label: "Dashboard",
        icon: <BarChart3 size={18} />,
        roles: ["master", "gestor"],
        requiresCompany: true,
      },
      {
        id: "devices",
        path: "/devices",
        label: "Dispositivos ADB",
        icon: <Smartphone size={18} />,
        roles: ["master", "gestor", "colaborador"],
        requiresCompany: true,
      },
      {
        id: "campaigns",
        path: "/campaigns",
        label: "Campanhas",
        icon: <Megaphone size={18} />,
        roles: ["master", "gestor"],
        requiresCompany: true,
      },
      {
        id: "automations",
        path: "/automations",
        label: "Automações",
        icon: <Bot size={18} />,
        roles: ["master", "gestor"],
        requiresCompany: true,
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    groupName: "SISTEMA",
    items: [
      {
        id: "logs",
        path: "/logs",
        label: "Logs do Engine",
        icon: <ScrollText size={18} />,
        roles: ["master", "gestor", "colaborador"],
        requiresCompany: true,
      },
      {
        id: "settings",
        path: "/settings",
        label: "Configurações",
        icon: <Settings size={18} />,
        roles: ["master", "gestor"],
        requiresCompany: true,
      },
    ],
  },
];

export default function Sidebar() {
  const { user, selectedCompanyId, leaveCompany, logout } = useAuth();
  const { hasPermission } = usePermissions();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  if (!user) return null;

  const insideCompany = selectedCompanyId !== null;
  const selectedCompany = MOCK_COMPANIES.find((c) => c.id === selectedCompanyId);

  const handleBackToCompanies = () => {
    leaveCompany();
    navigate("/companies");
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    navigate("/login");
  };

  return (
    <aside style={{ width: "256px" }} className="fixed left-0 top-0 bottom-0 flex flex-col z-40 bg-sidebar border-r border-border shadow-xl">
      {/* Brand Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 h-16 border-b border-border">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 shadow-md shadow-violet-500/30">
            <Zap size={20} className="text-white" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-base font-extrabold text-primary tracking-tight">Esfera</h1>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-violet-400 font-semibold tracking-wider">v3.0 HIGH PERFORMANCE</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Empresa Ativa Widget */}
      {insideCompany && selectedCompany && (
        <div className="mx-3 mt-3 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-border">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
              <Building2 size={16} className="text-violet-400 shrink-0" />
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary truncate">{selectedCompany.name}</span>
            </div>
            {user.role === "master" && (
              <button
                onClick={handleBackToCompanies}
                className="p-1 rounded-lg text-tertiary hover:text-violet-400 hover:bg-violet-500/10 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                title="Voltar às Empresas"
              >
                <ChevronLeft size={16} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Navigation List — Filtrado por role + permissões */}
      <nav className="flex-1 px-3 py-4 space-y-5 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
        {NAV_GROUPS.map((group) => {
          const visibleItems = group.items.filter((item) => {
            if (!item.roles.includes(user.role)) return false;
            if (item.requiresCompany && !insideCompany) return false;
            if (item.id === "companies" && insideCompany) return false;
            if (item.permissionKey && !hasPermission(item.permissionKey)) return false;
            return true;
          });

          if (visibleItems.length === 0) return null;

          return (
            <div key={group.groupName}>
              <h3 className="px-3 text-[10px] font-bold text-tertiary uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                {group.groupName}
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {visibleItems.map((item) => {
                  const isActive = location.pathname === item.path;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={item.id}
                      onClick={() => navigate(item.path)}
                      className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                        isActive
                          ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600/20 to-indigo-600/20 text-violet-300 font-semibold border border-violet-500/30 shadow-sm"
                          : "text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/5"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className={isActive ? "text-violet-400" : "text-tertiary"}>
                        {item.icon}
                      </span>
                      <span className="flex-1 text-left">{item.label}</span>
                      {item.badge && (
                        <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] rounded-md bg-violet-500/20 text-violet-300 font-mono">
                          {item.badge}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* Footer Profile & Logout */}
      <div className="border-t border-border p-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 text-white font-bold text-xs shrink-0">
              {user.displayName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary truncate">{user.displayName}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-tertiary capitalize">{user.role}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-tertiary hover:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/10 transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
            title="Sair"
          >
            <LogOut size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\StatusBar.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — StatusBar
// Barra inferior com status de conexão e info da empresa
// ============================================================

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { MOCK_COMPANIES, MOCK_DEVICES } from "../mock-data";
import { Wifi, WifiOff, Building2, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";

export default function StatusBar() {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();

  const company = MOCK_COMPANIES.find((c) => c.id === selectedCompanyId);
  const companyDevices = MOCK_DEVICES.filter((d) => d.companyId === selectedCompanyId);
  const onlineDevices = companyDevices.filter((d) => d.status === "online");

  return (
    <footer style={{ left: "256px" }} className="fixed bottom-0 right-0 h-8 z-30 bg-sidebar border-t border-border flex items-center justify-between px-4 text-xs text-tertiary">

      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        {/* Status de conexão */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          {onlineDevices.length > 0 ? (
            <>
              <Wifi size={12} className="text-emerald-400" />
              <span className="text-emerald-400">Conectado</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <WifiOff size={12} className="text-slate-500" />
              <span>Desconectado</span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Empresa atual */}
        {company && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Building2 size={12} />
            <span>{company.name}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        {/* Dispositivos online */}
        {selectedCompanyId && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Smartphone size={12} />
            <span>{onlineDevices.length}/{companyDevices.length} online</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Versão */}
        <span className="opacity-50">Esfera v3.0</span>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\ui\Badge.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Componente Badge
// Badges de status com cores e dot animado
// ============================================================

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

type BadgeVariant = "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "info" | "neutral" | "purple";

interface BadgeProps {
  variant?: BadgeVariant;
  children: ReactNode;
  dot?: boolean;
  pulse?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

const variantClasses: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
  success: "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30",
  warning: "bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30",
  danger: "bg-red-500/15 text-red-400 border-red-500/30",
  info: "bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30",
  neutral: "bg-slate-500/15 text-slate-400 border-slate-500/30",
  purple: "bg-violet-500/15 text-violet-400 border-violet-500/30",
};

const dotColors: Record<BadgeVariant, string> = {
  success: "bg-emerald-400",
  warning: "bg-amber-400",
  danger: "bg-red-400",
  info: "bg-blue-400",
  neutral: "bg-slate-400",
  purple: "bg-violet-400",
};

export default function Badge({
  variant = "neutral",
  children,
  dot = false,
  pulse = false,
  className = "",
}: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs
        font-medium border ${variantClasses[variant]} ${className}`}
    >
      {dot && (
        <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
          {pulse && (
            <span
              className={`absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full opacity-75
                animate-ping ${dotColors[variant]}`}
            />
          )}
          <span className={`relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 ${dotColors[variant]}`} />
        </span>
      )}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\ui\Button.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Componente Button
// Botão com variantes, loading state e ícones
// ============================================================

import { type ReactNode, type ButtonHTMLAttributes } from "react";
import { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";

type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "ghost" | "success";
type ButtonSize = "sm" | "md" | "lg";

interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
}

const variantClasses: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  primary: `bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 via-purple-600 to-indigo-600 text-white
    hover:from-violet-500 hover:via-purple-500 hover:to-indigo-500 shadow-xl shadow-violet-600/30
    hover:shadow-violet-600/40 active:scale-[0.98]`,
  secondary: `bg-white/5 text-secondary border border-white/10
    hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-primary hover:border-white/20 active:scale-[0.98]`,
  danger: `bg-red-500/15 text-red-300 border border-red-500/30
    hover:bg-red-500/25 hover:border-red-500/50 active:scale-[0.98]`,
  ghost: `text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/5 active:scale-[0.98]`,
  success: `bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30
    hover:bg-emerald-500/25 hover:border-emerald-500/50 active:scale-[0.98]`,
};

const sizeClasses: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  sm: "px-3 py-1.5 text-xs gap-1.5 rounded-xl font-semibold",
  md: "px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold gap-2 rounded-xl",
  lg: "px-6 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold gap-2 rounded-xl",
};

export default function Button({
  variant = "primary",
  size = "md",
  loading = false,
  icon,
  children,
  disabled,
  className = "",
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center font-medium rounded-xl
        transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer
        disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed
        ${variantClasses[variant]} ${sizeClasses[size]} ${className}`}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      {...props}
    >
      {loading ? (
        <Loader2 size={size === "sm" ? 14 : 16} className="animate-spin" />
      ) : (
        icon
      )}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\ui\Card.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Componente Card (Refatorado)
// Container com glassmorphism, suporte a hover glow e transições
// ============================================================

import type { ReactNode, HTMLAttributes } from "react";

interface CardProps extends HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {
  children: ReactNode;
  hover?: boolean;
  glow?: boolean;
  padding?: "none" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg";
}

const paddingClasses: Record<string, string> = {
  none: "",
  xs: "p-3",
  sm: "p-4",
  md: "p-5 sm:p-6",
  lg: "p-6 sm:p-8",
};

export default function Card({
  children,
  hover = false,
  glow = false,
  padding = "md",
  className = "",
  ...props
}: CardProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`rounded-2xl glass-panel relative overflow-hidden transition-all duration-300 ${
        hover
          ? "hover:border-violet-500/40 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-2xl hover:shadow-violet-500/15 cursor-pointer"
          : ""
      } ${glow ? "border-violet-500/40 shadow-xl shadow-violet-500/20" : ""} ${
        paddingClasses[padding]
      } ${className}`}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\ui\Modal.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Componente Modal
// Modal base com overlay, animações e suporte a Save/Cancel
// ============================================================

import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl";
}

const sizeClasses: Record<string, string> = {
  sm: "max-w-md",
  md: "max-w-lg",
  lg: "max-w-2xl",
  xl: "max-w-4xl",
};

export default function Modal({ isOpen, onClose, title, children, footer, size = "md" }: ModalProps) {
  const overlayRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
    };
    if (isOpen) {
      document.addEventListener("keydown", handleEsc);
      document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    }
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleEsc);
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      ref={overlayRef}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in"
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === overlayRef.current) onClose();
      }}
    >
      {/* Overlay */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" />

      {/* Modal Content */}
      <div
        className={`relative w-full ${sizeClasses[size]} rounded-2xl border
          bg-card border-border shadow-2xl animate-scale-in`}
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-border">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-primary">{title}</h2>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-secondary hover:text-primary
              hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
          {children}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        {footer && (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 px-6 py-4 border-t border-border">
            {footer}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\ui\ToastContainer.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Componente de Notificações Toast (ToastContainer.tsx)
// Pilha de notificações no canto inferior direito
// ============================================================

import { CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, XCircle, Info, X } from "lucide-react";

export interface ToastMessage {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  variant: "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "info";
}

interface ToastContainerProps {
  toasts: ToastMessage[];
  onDismiss: (id: string) => void;
}

export default function ToastContainer({ toasts, onDismiss }: ToastContainerProps) {
  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 flex flex-col space-y-2.5 max-w-sm w-full pointer-events-none">
      {toasts.map((t) => {
        let border = "border-violet-500/30 bg-slate-900/90 text-violet-300";
        let Icon = Info;

        if (t.variant === "success") {
          border = "border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-950/90 text-emerald-300";
          Icon = CheckCircle2;
        } else if (t.variant === "warning") {
          border = "border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-950/90 text-amber-300";
          Icon = AlertTriangle;
        } else if (t.variant === "danger") {
          border = "border-red-500/40 bg-red-950/90 text-red-300";
          Icon = XCircle;
        }

        return (
          <div
            key={t.id}
            className={`pointer-events-auto flex items-start justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl border backdrop-blur-md shadow-2xl animate-fade-in ${border}`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <Icon size={18} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
              <div>
                <h5 className="font-bold text-xs text-white">{t.title}</h5>
                {t.description && <p className="text-[11px] opacity-90 mt-0.5 leading-tight">{t.description}</p>}
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => onDismiss(t.id)}
              className="p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 text-tertiary hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0 ml-2"
            >
              <X size={14} />
            </button>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\components\ui\Toggle.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Componente Toggle
// Toggle switch animado para booleanos
// ============================================================

interface ToggleProps {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
  label?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  size?: "sm" | "md";
}

export default function Toggle({
  checked,
  onChange,
  label,
  disabled = false,
  size = "md",
}: ToggleProps) {
  const trackSize = size === "sm" ? "w-9 h-5" : "w-11 h-6";
  const thumbSize = size === "sm" ? "h-3.5 w-3.5" : "h-4 w-4";
  const thumbTranslate = size === "sm"
    ? (checked ? "translate-x-4.5" : "translate-x-0.5")
    : (checked ? "translate-x-5.5" : "translate-x-0.5");

  return (
    <label className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 ${disabled ? "opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed" : "cursor-pointer"}`}>
      <button
        role="switch"
        type="button"
        aria-checked={checked}
        disabled={disabled}
        onClick={() => !disabled && onChange(!checked)}
        className={`relative inline-flex items-center shrink-0 rounded-full
          transition-colors duration-300 ease-in-out focus:outline-none
          ${trackSize}
          ${checked
            ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600"
            : "bg-slate-600"
          }`}
      >
        <span
          className={`inline-block rounded-full bg-white shadow-lg
            transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out
            ${thumbSize} ${thumbTranslate}`}
        />
      </button>
      {label && (
        <span className="text-sm text-secondary select-none">{label}</span>
      )}
    </label>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\context\AuthContext.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Contexto de Autenticação (Tauri IPC + Fallback)
// Gerencia login nativo em Rust via SQLite, controle de roles
// e permissões granulares por usuário
// ============================================================

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";
import type { User, SessionState, Permission, SectorPermission } from "../types";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";

interface AuthContextType extends SessionState {
  login: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  logout: () => void;
  enterCompany: (companyId: number) => void;
  leaveCompany: () => void;
  error: string | null;
  permissions: Permission[];
  sectorPermissions: SectorPermission[];
  hasPermission: (key: string) => boolean;
  hasSectorPermission: (sector: string, type: "view" | "configure") => boolean;
  refreshPermissions: () => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | null>(null);

// Permissões padrão para master (acesso total)
const MASTER_DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS: Permission[] = [
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_all_tabs", enabled: true },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_edit_devices", enabled: true },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_manage_users", enabled: true },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_dashboard", enabled: true },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "is_active", enabled: true },
];

const MASTER_DEFAULT_SECTOR_PERMISSIONS: SectorPermission[] = [
  { userId: 0, sector: "junior", canView: true, canConfigure: true },
  { userId: 0, sector: "senior", canView: true, canConfigure: true },
  { userId: 0, sector: "pesquisa", canView: true, canConfigure: true },
  { userId: 0, sector: "pa_fixa_1", canView: true, canConfigure: true },
  { userId: 0, sector: "pa_fixa_2", canView: true, canConfigure: true },
  { userId: 0, sector: "comercial", canView: true, canConfigure: true },
];

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [selectedCompanyId, setSelectedCompanyId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [permissions, setPermissions] = useState<Permission[]>([]);
  const [sectorPermissions, setSectorPermissions] = useState<SectorPermission[]>([]);

  const loadPermissions = useCallback(async (userId: number, role: string) => {
    // Master sempre tem todas as permissões
    if (role === "master") {
      setPermissions(MASTER_DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS);
      setSectorPermissions(MASTER_DEFAULT_SECTOR_PERMISSIONS);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const [perms, sectorPerms] = await Promise.all([
        invoke<Permission[]>("get_user_permissions", { userId }),
        invoke<SectorPermission[]>("get_sector_permissions", { userId }),
      ]);
      setPermissions(perms);
      setSectorPermissions(sectorPerms);
    } catch {
      // Fallback: permissões básicas para gestor
      setPermissions([
        { userId, permissionKey: "allow_all_tabs", enabled: true },
        { userId, permissionKey: "allow_edit_devices", enabled: true },
        { userId, permissionKey: "allow_manage_users", enabled: false },
        { userId, permissionKey: "allow_dashboard", enabled: true },
        { userId, permissionKey: "is_active", enabled: true },
      ]);
      setSectorPermissions(MASTER_DEFAULT_SECTOR_PERMISSIONS);
    }
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(async (username: string, password: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    setError(null);

    try {
      const resUser = await invoke<User>("login", { username, password });
      setUser(resUser);
      if (resUser.role !== "master" && resUser.companyId) {
        setSelectedCompanyId(resUser.companyId);
      }
      await loadPermissions(resUser.id, resUser.role);
      return true;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      console.warn("Tauri invoke login não disponível ou erro (modo navegador/preview):", err);
      
      const SYSTEM_USERS: Record<string, { pass: string; user: User }> = {
        "vicenzzo.master": {
          pass: "root123",
          user: { id: 10, username: "vicenzzo.master", displayName: "Vicenzzo Master", role: "master", companyId: null },
        },
        "vicenzzo.mastronikolis": {
          pass: "Ope@2025",
          user: { id: 11, username: "vicenzzo.mastronikolis", displayName: "Vicenzzo Mastronikolis", role: "gestor", companyId: 1 },
        },
        "admin": {
          pass: "admin",
          user: { id: 1, username: "admin", displayName: "Administrador", role: "master", companyId: null },
        },
        "gestor": {
          pass: "gestor",
          user: { id: 2, username: "gestor", displayName: "Carlos Gestor", role: "gestor", companyId: 1 },
        },
        "colab": {
          pass: "colab",
          user: { id: 3, username: "colab", displayName: "Ana Operadora", role: "colaborador", companyId: 1 },
        },
      };

      const account = SYSTEM_USERS[username];
      if (account && account.pass === password) {
        setUser(account.user);
        if (account.user.role !== "master" && account.user.companyId) {
          setSelectedCompanyId(account.user.companyId);
        }
        await loadPermissions(account.user.id, account.user.role);
        return true;
      }

      setError("Usuário ou senha inválidos");
      return false;
    }
  }, [loadPermissions]);

  const logout = useCallback(() => {
    setUser(null);
    setSelectedCompanyId(null);
    setError(null);
    setPermissions([]);
    setSectorPermissions([]);
  }, []);

  const enterCompany = useCallback((companyId: number) => {
    setSelectedCompanyId(companyId);
  }, []);

  const leaveCompany = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedCompanyId(null);
  }, []);

  const hasPermission = useCallback((key: string): boolean => {
    if (!user) return false;
    if (user.role === "master") return true;
    const perm = permissions.find((p) => p.permissionKey === key);
    return perm?.enabled ?? false;
  }, [user, permissions]);

  const hasSectorPermission = useCallback((sector: string, type: "view" | "configure"): boolean => {
    if (!user) return false;
    if (user.role === "master") return true;
    const sp = sectorPermissions.find((s) => s.sector === sector);
    if (!sp) return false;
    return type === "view" ? sp.canView : sp.canConfigure;
  }, [user, sectorPermissions]);

  const refreshPermissions = useCallback(async () => {
    if (user) {
      await loadPermissions(user.id, user.role);
    }
  }, [user, loadPermissions]);

  // Carrega permissões se já há user (ex: refresh da página)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      loadPermissions(user.id, user.role);
    }
  }, [user, loadPermissions]);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        loggedIn: !!user,
        user,
        selectedCompanyId,
        login,
        logout,
        enterCompany,
        leaveCompany,
        error,
        permissions,
        sectorPermissions,
        hasPermission,
        hasSectorPermission,
        refreshPermissions,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextType {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useAuth deve ser usado dentro de um AuthProvider");
  }
  return context;
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\context\ThemeContext.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Contexto de Tema (Dark/Light Mode)
// Persiste preferência no localStorage
// ============================================================

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, type ReactNode } from "react";

type Theme = "dark" | "light";

interface ThemeContextType {
  theme: Theme;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
  isDark: boolean;
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType | null>(null);

function getInitialTheme(): Theme {
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem("esfera-theme");
    if (stored === "dark" || stored === "light") return stored;
  }
  return "dark"; // default
}

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(getInitialTheme);

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    root.classList.remove("dark", "light");
    root.classList.add(theme);
    localStorage.setItem("esfera-theme", theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = useCallback(() => {
    setTheme((prev) => (prev === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"));
  }, []);

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, toggleTheme, isDark: theme === "dark" }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme(): ThemeContextType {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useTheme deve ser usado dentro de um ThemeProvider");
  }
  return context;
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\hooks\useAudioAlarm.ts
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Hook de Alarme Sonoro (Web Audio API)
// Sintetizador nativo para avisos de Liberação de Chip e Alertas
// ============================================================

import { useCallback } from "react";

export function useAudioAlarm() {
  const playChipLiberatedSound = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AudioCtx) return;

      const ctx = new AudioCtx();
      const now = ctx.currentTime;

      // Sequência de notas harmoniosas de vitória (C5 -> E5 -> G5 -> C6)
      const notes = [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5];

      notes.forEach((freq, idx) => {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();

        osc.type = "sine";
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now + idx * 0.12);

        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now + idx * 0.12);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + idx * 0.12 + 0.3);

        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);

        osc.start(now + idx * 0.12);
        osc.stop(now + idx * 0.12 + 0.35);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Web Audio API indisponível:", e);
    }
  }, []);

  const playWarningAlarmSound = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AudioCtx) return;

      const ctx = new AudioCtx();
      const now = ctx.currentTime;

      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();

      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(440, now);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, now + 0.2);

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.25);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);

      osc.start(now);
      osc.stop(now + 0.3);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Web Audio API indisponível:", e);
    }
  }, []);

  return { playChipLiberatedSound, playWarningAlarmSound };
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\hooks\usePermissions.ts
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Hook de Permissões Granulares
// Facilita verificação de permissões em qualquer componente
// ============================================================

import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

export function usePermissions() {
  const { user, permissions, sectorPermissions, hasPermission, hasSectorPermission } = useAuth();

  const isMaster = user?.role === "master";
  const isGestor = user?.role === "gestor";
  const isColaborador = user?.role === "colaborador";

  const canEditDevices = hasPermission("allow_edit_devices");
  const canManageUsers = hasPermission("allow_manage_users");
  const canAccessDashboard = hasPermission("allow_dashboard");
  const canSeeAllTabs = hasPermission("allow_all_tabs");
  const isActive = hasPermission("is_active");

  const canViewSector = (sector: string) => hasSectorPermission(sector, "view");
  const canConfigureSector = (sector: string) => hasSectorPermission(sector, "configure");

  return {
    user,
    isMaster,
    isGestor,
    isColaborador,
    permissions,
    sectorPermissions,
    canEditDevices,
    canManageUsers,
    canAccessDashboard,
    canSeeAllTabs,
    isActive,
    canViewSector,
    canConfigureSector,
    hasPermission,
    hasSectorPermission,
  } as const;
}

// Esfera v3 — Automations Tab com Triggers & Actions

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

  set_chip_status: "bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 border-red-500/30",
  send_to_sector: "bg-pink-500/20 text-pink-400 border-pink-500/30",
};

const SECTORS = ["junior", "senior", "pesquisa", "pa_fixa_1", "pa_fixa_2", "comercial"];
const SECTOR_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  junior: "Junior",
  senior: "Senior",
  pesquisa: "Pesquisa",
  pa_fixa_1: "PA Fixa 1",
  pa_fixa_2: "PA Fixa 2",
  comercial: "Comercial",
};

export default function AutomationsTab() {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [editModalOpen, setEditModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedAutomation, setSelectedAutomation] = useState<Automation | null>(null);
  const [automationsList, setAutomationsList] = useState<Automation[]>([]);

  // Form states
  const [ruleName, setRuleName] = useState("");
  const [triggerType, setTriggerType] = useState<AutomationTriggerType>("keyword");
  const [actionType, setActionType] = useState<AutomationActionType>("send_response");
  const [ruleKeyword, setRuleKeyword] = useState("");
  const [ruleResponse, setRuleResponse] = useState("");
  const [ruleSector, setRuleSector] = useState("junior");
  const [scheduleTime, setScheduleTime] = useState("09:00");
  const [scheduleDays, setScheduleDays] = useState("1,2,3,4,5");
  const [targetCampaignId, setTargetCampaignId] = useState("");
  const [chipTargetStatus, setChipTargetStatus] = useState("restrito_24h");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    invoke<Automation[]>("get_automations", { companyId: selectedCompanyId })
      .then((data) => setAutomationsList(data))
      .catch(() => setAutomationsList(MOCK_AUTOMATIONS.filter((a) => a.companyId === selectedCompanyId)));
  }, [selectedCompanyId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedAutomation) {
      setRuleName(selectedAutomation.name);
      setTriggerType(selectedAutomation.triggerType || "keyword");
      setActionType(selectedAutomation.actionType || "send_response");
      setRuleKeyword(selectedAutomation.keyword);
      setRuleResponse(selectedAutomation.response);
      setRuleSector(selectedAutomation.sector || "junior");
      // Parse config JSONs
      try {
        const tc = JSON.parse(selectedAutomation.triggerConfig || "{}");
        if (tc.time) setScheduleTime(tc.time);
        if (tc.days) setScheduleDays(tc.days);
      } catch { /* noop */ }
      try {
        const ac = JSON.parse(selectedAutomation.actionConfig || "{}");
        if (ac.campaignId) setTargetCampaignId(String(ac.campaignId));
        if (ac.chipStatus) setChipTargetStatus(ac.chipStatus);
      } catch { /* noop */ }
    } else {
      resetForm();
    }
  }, [selectedAutomation, editModalOpen]);

  const resetForm = () => {
    setRuleName("");
    setTriggerType("keyword");
    setActionType("send_response");
    setRuleKeyword("");
    setRuleResponse("");
    setRuleSector("junior");
    setScheduleTime("09:00");
    setScheduleDays("1,2,3,4,5");
    setTargetCampaignId("");
    setChipTargetStatus("restrito_24h");
  };

  const handleSaveAutomation = async () => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    const triggerConfig = triggerType === "schedule"
      ? JSON.stringify({ time: scheduleTime, days: scheduleDays })
      : "{}";
    const actionConfig = actionType === "toggle_campaign"
      ? JSON.stringify({ campaignId: targetCampaignId })
      : actionType === "set_chip_status"
      ? JSON.stringify({ chipStatus: chipTargetStatus })
      : "{}";

    const payload = {
      id: selectedAutomation?.id || 0,
      name: ruleName,
      keyword: ruleKeyword,
      response: ruleResponse,
      active: selectedAutomation?.active ?? true,
      triggeredCount: selectedAutomation?.triggeredCount || 0,
      companyId: selectedCompanyId,
      createdAt: selectedAutomation?.createdAt || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      triggerType,
      actionType,
      triggerConfig,
      actionConfig,
      sector: ruleSector,
    };

    try {
      await invoke("create_automation", { automation: payload });
      const updated = await invoke<Automation[]>("get_automations", { companyId: selectedCompanyId });
      setAutomationsList(updated);
    } catch {
      if (!selectedAutomation) {
        setAutomationsList((prev) => [
          ...prev,
          {
            ...payload,
            id: Date.now(),
          } as Automation,
        ]);
      } else {
        setAutomationsList((prev) =>
          prev.map((a) =>
            a.id === selectedAutomation.id
              ? { ...a, ...payload } as Automation
              : a
          )
        );
      }
    }
    setEditModalOpen(false);
  };

  const filteredAutomations = automationsList.filter(
    (a) =>
      a.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      a.keyword.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  const activeRulesCount = automationsList.filter((a) => a.active).length;
  const totalTriggers = automationsList.reduce((acc, a) => acc + (a.triggeredCount || 0), 0);

  const openEditModal = (automation?: Automation) => {
    setSelectedAutomation(automation || null);
    setEditModalOpen(true);
  };

  const toggleActive = async (id: number, currentActive: boolean) => {
    const newActive = !currentActive;
    setAutomationsList((prev) =>
      prev.map((a) => (a.id === id ? { ...a, active: newActive } : a))
    );
    try {
      await invoke("update_automation", {
        automation: automationsList.find((a) => a.id === id),
      });
    } catch { /* noop */ }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id: number) => {
    try {
      await invoke("soft_delete_automation", { automationId: id });
    } catch { /* noop */ }
    setAutomationsList((prev) => prev.filter((a) => a.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">Automações Avançadas</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mt-0.5">
            Triggers inteligentes com ações automáticas — detections, agendamentos e respostas.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button icon={<Plus size={16} />} onClick={() => openEditModal()}>
          Nova Automação
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* KPI Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-500/15 border border-violet-500/20 text-violet-400">
            <Bot size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{automationsList.length}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Total de Regras</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{activeRulesCount}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Regras Ativas</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-400">
            <Zap size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{totalTriggers.toLocaleString()}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Total de Disparos</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/15 border border-blue-500/20 text-blue-400">
            <AlertTriangle size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">
              {automationsList.filter((a) => a.triggerType === "ban_detected").length}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Watchdogs Ban</p>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Search */}
      <div className="relative max-w-md flex items-center">
        <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 text-tertiary pointer-events-none z-10" />
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Buscar por regra ou palavra-chave..."
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full input-with-icon-left"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Automations List */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {filteredAutomations.map((automation, idx) => (
          <Card key={automation.id || idx} padding="none" hover>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between p-4 gap-4">
              <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3.5">
                <div
                  className={`flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl border ${
                    automation.active
                      ? "bg-violet-600/20 text-violet-400 border-violet-500/30"
                      : "bg-white/5 text-tertiary border-border"
                  }`}
                >
                  {TRIGGER_ICONS[automation.triggerType || "keyword"]}
                </div>

                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 flex-wrap">
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-primary">{automation.name}</h3>
                    <Badge variant={automation.active ? "success" : "neutral"} dot>
                      {automation.active ? "Ativa" : "Inativa"}
                    </Badge>
                    <Badge variant="purple" className="text-[10px] gap-1">
                      {TRIGGER_ICONS[automation.triggerType || "keyword"]}
                      {TRIGGER_LABELS[automation.triggerType || "keyword"]}
                    </Badge>
                    <Badge variant="info" className="text-[10px] gap-1">
                      {ACTION_ICONS[automation.actionType || "send_response"]}
                      {ACTION_LABELS[automation.actionType || "send_response"]}
                    </Badge>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-secondary flex-wrap">
                    {automation.keyword && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-violet-400 bg-violet-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-violet-500/20">
                        <Hash size={12} />
                        "{automation.keyword}"
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-tertiary">
                      <Zap size={12} className="text-amber-400" />
                      {automation.triggeredCount || 0} execuções
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mt-1.5 line-clamp-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <ArrowRight size={12} className="shrink-0 text-secondary" />
                    <span>
                      {automation.actionType === "send_response" && automation.response}
                      {automation.actionType === "move_chip" && `Mover chip → ${SECTOR_LABELS[automation.sector || "junior"]}`}
                      {automation.actionType === "notify" && "Notificar gestor via sistema"}
                      {automation.actionType === "toggle_campaign" && "Pausar/iniciar campanha vinculada"}
                      {automation.actionType === "set_chip_status" && "Alterar status do chip automaticamente"}
                      {automation.actionType === "send_to_sector" && `Encaminhar → ${SECTOR_LABELS[automation.sector || "junior"]}`}
                    </span>
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 border-t sm:border-t-0 border-border pt-3 sm:pt-0 shrink-0">
                <Toggle
                  checked={automation.active}
                  onChange={() => toggleActive(automation.id, automation.active)}
                  size="sm"
                />
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" icon={<Pencil size={14} />} onClick={() => openEditModal(automation)}>
                  Editar
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="sm"
                  icon={<Trash2 size={14} />}
                  className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-300"
                  onClick={() => handleDelete(automation.id)}
                >
                  Excluir
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {filteredAutomations.length === 0 && (
        <Card className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center text-tertiary">
          <Bot size={40} className="mb-2 opacity-30" />
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-secondary">Nenhuma automação encontrada</p>
          <p className="text-xs">Crie uma nova regra com triggers e ações automáticas.</p>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Modal Criar/Editar */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={editModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setEditModalOpen(false)}
        title={selectedAutomation ? `Editar: ${selectedAutomation.name}` : "Nova Automação"}
        size="lg"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setEditModalOpen(false)}>
              Cancelar
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSaveAutomation}>Salvar Automação</Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="space-y-5 text-xs">
          {/* Nome */}
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Nome da Automação</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={ruleName}
              onChange={(e) => setRuleName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Ex: Auto-resposta Orçamento"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          {/* TRIGGER */}
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border space-y-4">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-primary flex items-center gap-2">
              <Zap size={16} className="text-violet-400" />
              Gatilho (Trigger)
            </h4>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              {(Object.keys(TRIGGER_LABELS) as AutomationTriggerType[]).map((t) => (
                <button
                  key={t}
                  onClick={() => setTriggerType(t)}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-all cursor-pointer border ${
                    triggerType === t
                      ? `${TRIGGER_COLORS[t]} border-current`
                      : "bg-white/[0.02] text-secondary border-border hover:bg-white/[0.04]"
                  }`}
                >
                  {TRIGGER_ICONS[t]}
                  {TRIGGER_LABELS[t]}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Trigger-specific config */}
            {triggerType === "keyword" && (
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Palavra-chave</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={ruleKeyword}
                  onChange={(e) => setRuleKeyword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ex: orcamento, preco, valor"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            {triggerType === "schedule" && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Horário</label>
                  <input
                    type="time"
                    value={scheduleTime}
                    onChange={(e) => setScheduleTime(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Dias (1=Seg...7=Dom)</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={scheduleDays}
                    onChange={(e) => setScheduleDays(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="1,2,3,4,5"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {triggerType === "ban_detected" && (
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 text-red-300 text-xs">
                Esta automação será executada sempre que um chip for marcado como banido pelo WhatsApp.
              </div>
            )}

            {triggerType === "device_offline" && (
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 text-xs">
                Esta automação será executada quando um dispositivo ficar offline por mais de 5 minutos.
              </div>
            )}

            {triggerType === "message_received" && (
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 text-xs">
                Esta automação será executada ao receber qualquer mensagem de cliente.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* ACTION */}
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border space-y-4">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-primary flex items-center gap-2">
              <ArrowRight size={16} className="text-violet-400" />
              Ação
            </h4>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              {(Object.keys(ACTION_LABELS) as AutomationActionType[]).map((a) => (
                <button
                  key={a}
                  onClick={() => setActionType(a)}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-all cursor-pointer border ${
                    actionType === a
                      ? `${ACTION_COLORS[a]} border-current`
                      : "bg-white/[0.02] text-secondary border-border hover:bg-white/[0.04]"
                  }`}
                >
                  {ACTION_ICONS[a]}
                  {ACTION_LABELS[a]}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Action-specific config */}
            {actionType === "send_response" && (
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Mensagem de Resposta</label>
                <textarea
                  value={ruleResponse}
                  onChange={(e) => setRuleResponse(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Digite a resposta automática..."
                  rows={4}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all resize-none"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            {actionType === "move_chip" && (
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Mover para Setor</label>
                <select
                  value={ruleSector}
                  onChange={(e) => setRuleSector(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                >
                  {SECTORS.map((s) => (
                    <option key={s} value={s}>{SECTOR_LABELS[s]}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            )}

            {actionType === "notify" && (
              <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 text-xs">
                O gestor responsável receberá uma notificação no painel de alertas do sistema.
              </div>
            )}

            {actionType === "toggle_campaign" && (
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">ID da Campanha</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={targetCampaignId}
                  onChange={(e) => setTargetCampaignId(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ID da campanha"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                />
              </div>
            )}

            {actionType === "set_chip_status" && (
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Novo Status do Chip</label>
                <select
                  value={chipTargetStatus}
                  onChange={(e) => setChipTargetStatus(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                >
                  <option value="livre">Livre</option>
                  <option value="restrito_24h">Restrito 24h</option>
                  <option value="restrito_7d">Restrito 7d</option>
                  <option value="banido">Banido</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            )}

            {actionType === "send_to_sector" && (
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Encaminhar para Setor</label>
                <select
                  value={ruleSector}
                  onChange={(e) => setRuleSector(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                >
                  {SECTORS.map((s) => (
                    <option key={s} value={s}>{SECTOR_LABELS[s]}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\CampaignsTab.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Campaigns Tab (com Importador, Spintax & IA)
// Central de Campanhas, Importador de Contatos CSV e Editor
// ============================================================

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { MOCK_CAMPAIGNS } from "../mock-data";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import Modal from "../components/ui/Modal";
import Toggle from "../components/ui/Toggle";
import type { Campaign, CampaignStatus, Contact } from "../types";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import {
  Megaphone,
  Plus,
  Play,
  Pause,
  Pencil,
  Users,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  FileText,
  Clock,
  Smartphone,
  Brain,
  Upload,
  Sparkles,
  Send,
  Search,
  FileUp,
} from "lucide-react";

const statusConfig: Record<
  CampaignStatus,
  { variant: "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "info" | "neutral" | "purple"; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }
> = {
  running: { variant: "success", label: "Rodando", icon: <Play size={12} /> },
  paused: { variant: "warning", label: "Pausada", icon: <Pause size={12} /> },
  completed: { variant: "info", label: "Concluída", icon: <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> },
  draft: { variant: "neutral", label: "Rascunho", icon: <FileText size={12} /> },
  error: { variant: "danger", label: "Erro", icon: <AlertCircle size={12} /> },
};

export default function CampaignsTab() {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>("all");
  const [editModalOpen, setEditModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [contactsModalOpen, setContactsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedCampaign, setSelectedCampaign] = useState<Campaign | null>(null);
  const [campaignsList, setCampaignsList] = useState<Campaign[]>([]);

  // Import State
  const [importModalOpen, setImportModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [rawContactsInput, setRawContactsInput] = useState("");
  const [parsedContacts, setParsedContacts] = useState<{ name: string; phone: string }[]>([]);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    invoke<Campaign[]>("get_campaigns", { companyId: selectedCompanyId })
      .then((data) => setCampaignsList(data))
      .catch(() => setCampaignsList(MOCK_CAMPAIGNS.filter((c) => c.companyId === selectedCompanyId)));
  }, [selectedCompanyId]);

  const filteredCampaigns = campaignsList.filter((c) => {
    const matchesSearch = c.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    const matchesStatus = statusFilter === "all" || c.status === statusFilter;
    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  const runningCount = campaignsList.filter((c) => c.status === "running").length;
  const totalSent = campaignsList.reduce((acc, c) => acc + (c.sentCount || 0), 0);
  const totalContacts = campaignsList.reduce((acc, c) => acc + (c.contactsCount || 0), 0);

  const openEditModal = (campaign?: Campaign) => {
    setSelectedCampaign(campaign || null);
    setEditModalOpen(true);
  };
