  const openContactsModal = (campaign: Campaign) => {
    setSelectedCampaign(campaign);
    setContactsModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Higieniza números no padrão REALESS / Esfera v3
  const sanitizePhoneNumber = (raw: string) => {
    let clean = raw.replace(/\D/g, "");
    if (clean.length === 11 && clean.startsWith("9")) {
      clean = "55" + clean;
    } else if (clean.length === 9) {
      clean = "5511" + clean;
    }
    if (clean.length > 0 && !clean.startsWith("+")) {
      clean = "+" + clean;
    }
    return clean;
  };

  const handleParseContacts = () => {
    const lines = rawContactsInput.split("\n");
    const list: { name: string; phone: string }[] = [];
    for (const line of lines) {
      if (!line.trim()) continue;
      const parts = line.split(/[,;\t]/);
      let name = "Cliente";
      let rawPhone = line;
      if (parts.length >= 2) {
        name = parts[0].trim();
        rawPhone = parts[1].trim();
      }
      const phone = sanitizePhoneNumber(rawPhone);
      if (phone.length > 5) {
        list.push({ name, phone });
      }
    }
    setParsedContacts(list);
  };

  const handleFileUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (ev) => {
      const text = ev.target?.result as string;
      setRawContactsInput(text);
      // Auto-parse after load
      const lines = text.split("\n");
      const list: { name: string; phone: string }[] = [];
      for (const line of lines) {
        if (!line.trim()) continue;
        const parts = line.split(/[,;\t]/);
        let name = "Cliente";
        let rawPhone = line;
        if (parts.length >= 2) {
          name = parts[0].trim();
          rawPhone = parts[1].trim();
        }
        const phone = sanitizePhoneNumber(rawPhone);
        if (phone.length > 5) {
          list.push({ name, phone });
        }
      }
      setParsedContacts(list);
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    // Reset input so same file can be re-selected
    e.target.value = "";
  };

  const handleConfirmImport = async () => {
    if (!selectedCampaign || parsedContacts.length === 0) return;
    try {
      await invoke("import_campaign_contacts", {
        campaignId: selectedCampaign.id,
        contacts: parsedContacts.map((c) => [c.name, c.phone]),
      });
    } catch {
      console.warn("Importação persistida em lote (Offline/Mock)");
    }

    setCampaignsList((prev) =>
      prev.map((c) =>
        c.id === selectedCampaign.id
          ? { ...c, contactsCount: c.contactsCount + parsedContacts.length }
          : c
      )
    );

    setImportModalOpen(false);
    setRawContactsInput("");
    setParsedContacts([]);
  };

  // Campaign dispatch controls
  const handleStartCampaign = async (campaign: Campaign) => {
    try {
      await invoke("start_campaign", { campaignId: campaign.id });
    } catch { /* noop */ }
    setCampaignsList((prev) =>
      prev.map((c) => (c.id === campaign.id ? { ...c, status: "running" as const } : c))
    );
  };

  const handlePauseCampaign = async (campaign: Campaign) => {
    try {
      await invoke("pause_campaign", { campaignId: campaign.id });
    } catch { /* noop */ }
    setCampaignsList((prev) =>
      prev.map((c) => (c.id === campaign.id ? { ...c, status: "paused" as const } : c))
    );
  };

  const handleSendBatch = async (campaign: Campaign) => {
    try {
      const result = await invoke<{ sent_in_batch: number; remaining: number; campaign_complete: boolean }>(
        "send_campaign",
        { campaignId: campaign.id }
      );
      setCampaignsList((prev) =>
        prev.map((c) =>
          c.id === campaign.id
            ? {
                ...c,
                sentCount: (c.sentCount || 0) + result.sent_in_batch,
                status: result.campaign_complete ? "completed" as const : c.status,
              }
            : c
        )
      );
    } catch { /* noop */ }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      {/* Hidden CSV file input */}
      <input
        ref={fileInputRef}
        type="file"
        accept=".csv,.txt,.tsv"
        className="hidden"
        onChange={handleFileUpload}
      />

      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">Central de Campanhas</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mt-0.5">
            Crie disparos em massa, programe automações e personalize mensagens com IA.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button icon={<Plus size={16} />} onClick={() => openEditModal()}>
          Criar Nova Campanha
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* KPI Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-500/15 border border-violet-500/20 text-violet-400">
            <Megaphone size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{campaignsList.length}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Total de Campanhas</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400">
            <Play size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{runningCount}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Campanhas Rodando</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/15 border border-blue-500/20 text-blue-400">
            <Send size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{totalSent.toLocaleString()}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Disparos Concluídos</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-indigo-500/15 border border-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-400">
            <Users size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{totalContacts.toLocaleString()}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Fila de Contatos</p>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Search */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="relative w-full sm:w-80 flex items-center">
          <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 text-tertiary pointer-events-none z-10" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Buscar campanha por nome..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full input-with-icon-left"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto w-full sm:w-auto">
          {["all", "running", "paused", "completed", "draft"].map((status) => (
            <button
              key={status}
              onClick={() => setStatusFilter(status)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-all cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                statusFilter === status
                  ? "bg-violet-600/20 text-violet-300 border border-violet-500/30"
                  : "bg-white/5 text-secondary border border-border hover:bg-white/10"
              }`}
            >
              {status === "all" ? "Todas" : statusConfig[status as CampaignStatus]?.label || status}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid de Campanhas */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
        {filteredCampaigns.map((campaign) => (
          <CampaignCard
            key={campaign.id}
            campaign={campaign}
            onEdit={() => openEditModal(campaign)}
            onContacts={() => openContactsModal(campaign)}
            onImport={() => {
              setSelectedCampaign(campaign);
              setImportModalOpen(true);
            }}
            onStart={() => handleStartCampaign(campaign)}
            onPause={() => handlePauseCampaign(campaign)}
            onSendBatch={() => handleSendBatch(campaign)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Edit/Create Campaign Modal */}
      {editModalOpen && (
        <CampaignEditModal
          isOpen={editModalOpen}
          onClose={() => setEditModalOpen(false)}
          campaign={selectedCampaign}
          onSaved={(savedCamp) => {
            setCampaignsList((prev) => {
              const idx = prev.findIndex((c) => c.id === savedCamp.id);
              if (idx >= 0) {
                const updated = [...prev];
                updated[idx] = savedCamp;
                return updated;
              }
              return [savedCamp, ...prev];
            });
            if (selectedCompanyId) {
              invoke<Campaign[]>("get_campaigns", { companyId: selectedCompanyId })
                .then((data) => setCampaignsList(data))
                .catch(() => {});
            }
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Contacts List Modal */}
      {contactsModalOpen && (
        <ContactsModal
          isOpen={contactsModalOpen}
          onClose={() => setContactsModalOpen(false)}
          campaign={selectedCampaign}
          onOpenImport={() => setImportModalOpen(true)}
        />
      )}

      {/* Contact Import Modal */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={importModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setImportModalOpen(false)}
        title={selectedCampaign ? `Importar Lista: ${selectedCampaign.name}` : "Importar Contatos"}
        size="lg"
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setImportModalOpen(false)}>
              Cancelar
            </Button>
            <Button
              onClick={handleConfirmImport}
              disabled={parsedContacts.length === 0}
              icon={<Upload size={14} />}
            >
              Confirmar ({parsedContacts.length})
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
          {/* CSV File Upload */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border border-dashed">
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-500/15 border border-violet-500/20 text-violet-400">
              <FileUp size={20} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="font-semibold text-primary mb-0.5">Upload de arquivo CSV/TXT</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-tertiary">
                Formato: Nome, Telefone (um por linha). Separadores: vírgula, ponto e vírgula ou tab.
              </p>
            </div>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="secondary"
              icon={<Upload size={13} />}
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            >
              Selecionar Arquivo
            </Button>
          </div>

          <div className="text-center text-[10px] text-tertiary font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">— ou cole manualmente —</div>

          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">
              Cole os contatos (Formato: Nome, Telefone)
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={6}
              value={rawContactsInput}
              onChange={(e) => setRawContactsInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Maria Silva, 11999991111&#10;João Souza, 5511988882222&#10;5511977773333"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all resize-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">
              O importador limpa formatação e insere DDI +55 automaticamente.
            </span>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={handleParseContacts}>
              Processar Prévia
            </Button>
          </div>

          {parsedContacts.length > 0 && (
            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
              <span className="font-bold text-primary block mb-2">Prévia ({parsedContacts.length} contatos):</span>
              <div className="max-h-36 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar space-y-1.5 pr-1">
                {parsedContacts.map((c, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="flex justify-between items-center text-[11px] py-1 border-b border-border last:border-b-0">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-secondary">{c.name}</span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-violet-300">{c.phone}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

// ---- Campaign Card Component ----
function CampaignCard({
  campaign,
  onEdit,
  onContacts,
  onImport,
  onStart,
  onPause,
  onSendBatch,
}: {
  campaign: Campaign;
  onEdit: () => void;
  onContacts: () => void;
  onImport: () => void;
  onStart: () => void;
  onPause: () => void;
  onSendBatch: () => void;
}) {
  const status = statusConfig[campaign.status as CampaignStatus] || statusConfig.draft;
  const contactsCount = campaign.contactsCount || 0;
  const sentCount = campaign.sentCount || 0;
  const failedCount = campaign.failedCount || 0;
  const progress = contactsCount > 0 ? Math.round((sentCount / contactsCount) * 100) : 0;
  const devices = campaign.deviceIds || [];

  return (
    <Card padding="md" hover className="flex flex-col justify-between h-[340px]">
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-4">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-primary leading-tight line-clamp-1">{campaign.name}</h3>
          <Badge variant={status.variant}>{status.label}</Badge>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border mb-4">
          <p className="text-xs text-secondary line-clamp-2">
            {campaign.greeting && <span className="text-violet-400 font-medium">{campaign.greeting} </span>}
            {campaign.message}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-4">
          <div className="text-center p-2 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
            <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary mb-0.5">Contatos</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-primary">{contactsCount.toLocaleString()}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="text-center p-2 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
            <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary mb-0.5">Enviados</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-400">{sentCount.toLocaleString()}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="text-center p-2 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
            <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary mb-0.5">Falhas</p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-red-400">{failedCount}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-4">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-tertiary mb-1">
            <span>Progresso</span>
            <span className="font-semibold text-primary">{progress}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2 rounded-full bg-white/5 overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ${
                campaign.status === "completed"
                  ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-400"
                  : "bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-500"
              }`}
              style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 flex-wrap">
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-tertiary">
            <Smartphone size={13} />
            {devices.length || 1} cel.
          </span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-tertiary">
            <Clock size={13} />
            {campaign.delayMin || 30}-{campaign.delayMax || 90}s
          </span>
          {campaign.useSpintax && (
            <Badge variant="purple" className="text-[10px]">Spintax</Badge>
          )}
          {campaign.useAI && (
            <Badge variant="info" className="text-[10px]">
              <Brain size={11} className="mr-0.5" /> IA
            </Badge>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-2">
        {campaign.status === "running" ? (
          <>
            <Button variant="danger" size="sm" icon={<Pause size={13} />} onClick={onPause}>
              Pausar
            </Button>
            <Button variant="success" size="sm" icon={<Send size={13} />} onClick={onSendBatch}>
              Enviar
            </Button>
          </>
        ) : campaign.status === "paused" ? (
          <>
            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" icon={<Play size={13} />} onClick={onStart}>
              Retomar
            </Button>
            <Button variant="success" size="sm" icon={<Send size={13} />} onClick={onSendBatch}>
              Enviar
            </Button>
          </>
        ) : campaign.status === "completed" ? (
          <Badge variant="success" className="gap-1">
            <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> Concluída
          </Badge>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" icon={<Play size={13} />} onClick={onStart}>
              Iniciar
            </Button>
            <Button variant="success" size="sm" icon={<Send size={13} />} onClick={onSendBatch}>
              Enviar
            </Button>
          </>
        )}
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon={<Upload size={13} />} onClick={onImport}>
          Importar
        </Button>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon={<Pencil size={13} />} onClick={onEdit}>
          Editar
        </Button>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon={<Users size={13} />} onClick={onContacts}>
          Fila
        </Button>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

// ---- Campaign Edit Modal ----
function CampaignEditModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  campaign,
  onSaved,
}: {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  campaign: Campaign | null;
  onSaved: (campaign: Campaign) => void;
}) {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();
  const [useSpintax, setUseSpintax] = useState(campaign?.useSpintax ?? true);
  const [useAI, setUseAI] = useState(campaign?.useAI ?? false);

  const [nameText, setNameText] = useState(campaign?.name || "");
  const [greetingText, setGreetingText] = useState(campaign?.greeting || "");
  const [bodyText, setBodyText] = useState(campaign?.message || "");
  const [closingText, setClosingText] = useState(campaign?.closing || "");
  const [delayMinVal, setDelayMinVal] = useState(campaign?.delayMin || 30);
  const [delayMaxVal, setDelayMaxVal] = useState(campaign?.delayMax || 90);
  const [aiModelVal, setAiModelVal] = useState(campaign?.aiModel || "gpt-4o-mini");
  const [aiPromptVal, setAiPromptVal] = useState(campaign?.aiPrompt || "");
  const [spintaxPreview, setSpintaxPreview] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setNameText(campaign?.name || "");
      setGreetingText(campaign?.greeting || "{Olá|Oi|Bom dia} {nome}, tudo bem?");
      setBodyText(campaign?.message || "Temos uma condição especial disponível para você hoje!");
      setClosingText(campaign?.closing || "Atenciosamente, {Equipe REALESS|Setor Financeiro}");
      setDelayMinVal(campaign?.delayMin || 30);
      setDelayMaxVal(campaign?.delayMax || 90);
      setUseSpintax(campaign?.useSpintax ?? true);
      setUseAI(campaign?.useAI ?? false);
      setAiModelVal(campaign?.aiModel || "gpt-4o-mini");
      setAiPromptVal(campaign?.aiPrompt || "");
      setSpintaxPreview("");
    }
  }, [isOpen, campaign]);

  const handleSaveCampaign = async () => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    const finalName = nameText.trim() || `Campanha ${new Date().toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")} ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}`;
    const payload = {
      id: campaign?.id || 0,
      name: finalName,
      greeting: greetingText,
      message: bodyText,
      closing: closingText,
      delayMin: parseInt(String(delayMinVal), 10) || 30,
      delayMax: parseInt(String(delayMaxVal), 10) || 90,
      useSpintax,
      useAi: useAI,
      useAI,
      aiModel: aiModelVal,
      aiPrompt: aiPromptVal,
      status: campaign?.status || "draft",
      contactsCount: campaign?.contactsCount || 0,
      sentCount: campaign?.sentCount || 0,
      failedCount: campaign?.failedCount || 0,
      companyId: selectedCompanyId,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
    };

    try {
      if (campaign?.id) {
        await invoke("update_campaign", { campaign: payload });
      } else {
        const newId = await invoke<number>("create_campaign", { campaign: payload });
        if (newId) payload.id = newId;
      }
      onSaved(payload as unknown as Campaign);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("Criando/atualizando campanha no estado local:", err);
      const fallbackCamp: Campaign = {
        id: campaign?.id || Date.now(),
        name: finalName,
        greeting: greetingText,
        message: bodyText,
        closing: closingText,
        delayMin: parseInt(String(delayMinVal), 10) || 30,
        delayMax: parseInt(String(delayMaxVal), 10) || 90,
        useSpintax,
        useAI,
        aiModel: aiModelVal,
        aiPrompt: aiPromptVal,
        status: (campaign?.status || "draft") as CampaignStatus,
        contactsCount: campaign?.contactsCount || 0,
        sentCount: campaign?.sentCount || 0,
        failedCount: campaign?.failedCount || 0,
        companyId: selectedCompanyId,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      };
      onSaved(fallbackCamp);
      onClose();
    }
  };

  const handleTestSpintax = () => {
    const resolveSpintax = (text: string) => {
      let resolved = text;
      const spintaxRegex = /\{([^{}]+)\}/g;
      let match;
      while ((match = spintaxRegex.exec(resolved)) !== null) {
        const options = match[1].split("|");
        const chosen = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
        resolved = resolved.replace(match[0], chosen);
        spintaxRegex.lastIndex = 0;
      }
      return resolved;
    };
    const resolvedGreeting = resolveSpintax(greetingText);
    const resolvedBody = resolveSpintax(bodyText);
    const resolvedClosing = resolveSpintax(closingText);
    setSpintaxPreview(`${resolvedGreeting} ${resolvedBody} ${resolvedClosing}`.trim());
  };

  const handleTestAI = async () => {
    const msg = `${greetingText} ${bodyText} ${closingText}`.trim();
    try {
      const rewritten = await invoke<string>("rewrite_message_ai", { message: msg, contactName: "Maria" });
      setSpintaxPreview(rewritten);
    } catch {
      setSpintaxPreview("[IA Mock] " + msg.replace("{nome}", "Maria"));
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={campaign ? `Editar Campanha: ${campaign.name}` : "Criar Nova Campanha"}
      size="lg"
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancelar
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={handleSaveCampaign}>Salvar Campanha</Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
        <div>
          <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Nome da Campanha</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={nameText}
            onChange={(e) => setNameText(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex: Disparo Promocional Julho 2026"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Saudação (Spintax Suportado)</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={greetingText}
            onChange={(e) => setGreetingText(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex: {Olá|Bom dia|Oi} {nome}, tudo bem?"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Corpo da Mensagem (Spintax Suportado)</label>
          <textarea
            value={bodyText}
            onChange={(e) => setBodyText(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex: Identificamos um acordo {pendente|disponível} com {desconto|abatimento} de 50%."
            rows={4}
            className="resize-none"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Assinatura / Fechamento</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={closingText}
            onChange={(e) => setClosingText(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex: Atenciosamente, {Equipe REALESS|Setor Financeiro}"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Delay Mínimo (seg)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={delayMinVal}
              onChange={(e) => setDelayMinVal(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0)}
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Delay Máximo (seg)</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={delayMaxVal}
              onChange={(e) => setDelayMaxVal(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
          <Toggle checked={useSpintax} onChange={setUseSpintax} label="Habilitar Variação Spintax" />
          <Toggle checked={useAI} onChange={setUseAI} label="Personalização Inteligente via IA" />

          {useSpintax && (
            <div className="pt-2.5 space-y-2.5 animate-scale-in">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="font-semibold text-primary block">Testador de Variação</span>
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" icon={<Sparkles size={13} />} onClick={handleTestSpintax}>
                  Simular Spintax
                </Button>
              </div>
              {spintaxPreview && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-violet-600/10 border border-violet-500/25 text-violet-200 font-mono">
                  {spintaxPreview}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {useAI && (
            <div className="space-y-3 pl-4 pt-2 border-l-2 border-violet-500/40 animate-fade-in">
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Modelo de IA</label>
                <select
                  value={aiModelVal}
                  onChange={(e) => setAiModelVal(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
                >
                  <option value="gpt-4o-mini">GPT-4o Mini</option>
                  <option value="gpt-4o">GPT-4o High Speed</option>
                  <option value="claude-3-haiku">Claude 3 Haiku</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Prompt de Instrução</label>
                <textarea
                  value={aiPromptVal}
                  onChange={(e) => setAiPromptVal(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Instrua a IA sobre como reescrever a mensagem..."
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all resize-none"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                <span className="font-semibold text-primary block">Testar Reescrita IA</span>
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" icon={<Brain size={13} />} onClick={handleTestAI}>
                  Gerar com IA
                </Button>
              </div>
              {spintaxPreview && (
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-600/10 border border-blue-500/25 text-blue-200 font-mono">
                  {spintaxPreview}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

// ---- Contacts List Modal ----
function ContactsModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  campaign,
  onOpenImport,
}: {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  campaign: Campaign | null;
  onOpenImport: () => void;
}) {
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<Contact[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !campaign) return;
    invoke<Contact[]>("get_campaign_contacts", { campaignId: campaign.id })
      .then((data) => setContacts(data))
      .catch(() => {
        setContacts([
          {
            id: 1,
            name: "Maria Silva",
            phone: "+5511999991111",
            status: "pending" as any,
            campaignId: campaign.id,
          },
          {
            id: 2,
            name: "João Souza",
            phone: "+5511988882222",
            status: "sent" as any,
            campaignId: campaign.id,
          },
        ]);
      });
  }, [isOpen, campaign]);

  const pendingCount = contacts.filter((c) => c.status === "pending").length;
  const sentCount = contacts.filter((c) => c.status === "sent").length;

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={campaign ? `Fila de Disparos: ${campaign.name}` : "Fila de Contatos"}
      size="lg"
      footer={
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Fechar
          </Button>
          <Button variant="primary" icon={<Upload size={14} />} onClick={() => { onClose(); onOpenImport(); }}>
            Importar Contatos
          </Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {contacts.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs">
            <Badge variant="neutral">Pendentes: {pendingCount}</Badge>
            <Badge variant="success">Enviados: {sentCount}</Badge>
            <Badge variant="purple">Total: {contacts.length}</Badge>
          </div>
        )}
        {contacts.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-10 text-tertiary">
            <Users size={36} className="mx-auto mb-2 opacity-30" />
            <p className="text-xs font-medium">Nenhum contato cadastrado nesta campanha</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs border-collapse">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b border-border text-tertiary font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">
                <th className="py-2.5">Nome</th>
                <th className="py-2.5">Telefone</th>
                <th className="py-2.5">Status</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-border">
              {contacts.map((contact) => (
                <tr key={contact.id} className="hover:bg-white/[0.02]">
                  <td className="py-2.5 font-medium text-primary">{contact.contactName}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5 font-mono text-secondary">{contact.phone}</td>
                  <td className="py-2.5">
                    {contact.status === "sent" ? (
                      <Badge variant="success">Enviado</Badge>
                    ) : contact.status === "failed" ? (
                      <Badge variant="danger">Falhou</Badge>
                    ) : (
                      <Badge variant="neutral">Pendente</Badge>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\CompaniesTab.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Companies Tab (Layout Grid de Cards + Edição)
// Visão geral das empresas com botões de Gerenciar e Editar Empresa
// ============================================================

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { MOCK_COMPANIES } from "../mock-data";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import Modal from "../components/ui/Modal";
import Toggle from "../components/ui/Toggle";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import type { Company } from "../types";
import {
  Building2,
  Smartphone,
  Users,
  ArrowRight,
  Plus,
  CheckCircle2,
  Search,
  Pencil,
} from "lucide-react";

export default function CompaniesTab() {
  const { enterCompany } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [companiesList, setCompaniesList] = useState<Company[]>([]);

  // Modal State
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingCompany, setEditingCompany] = useState<Company | null>(null);
  const [editName, setEditName] = useState("");
  const [editDeviceLimit, setEditDeviceLimit] = useState(50);
  const [editUserLimit, setEditUserLimit] = useState(20);
  const [editActive, setEditActive] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    invoke<Company[]>("get_companies")
      .then((data) => {
        if (data && data.length > 0) {
          setCompaniesList(data);
        } else {
          setCompaniesList(MOCK_COMPANIES);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => setCompaniesList(MOCK_COMPANIES));
  }, []);

  const filteredCompanies = companiesList.filter((c) =>
    c.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  );

  const activeCompaniesCount = companiesList.filter((c) => c.status === "active").length;
  const totalDevicesAllocated = companiesList.reduce((acc, c) => acc + (c.deviceCount || 0), 0);
  const totalUsersAllocated = companiesList.reduce((acc, c) => acc + (c.userCount || 0), 0);

  const handleEnterCompany = (companyId: number) => {
    enterCompany(companyId);
    navigate("/devices");
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (company?: Company) => {
    if (company) {
      setEditingCompany(company);
      setEditName(company.name);
      setEditDeviceLimit(company.deviceLimit);
      setEditUserLimit(company.userLimit);
      setEditActive(company.status === "active");
    } else {
      setEditingCompany(null);
      setEditName("");
      setEditDeviceLimit(50);
      setEditUserLimit(20);
      setEditActive(true);
    }
    setModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSaveCompany = () => {
    if (!editName.trim()) return;

    if (editingCompany) {
      // Atualiza localmente
      setCompaniesList((prev) =>
        prev.map((c) =>
          c.id === editingCompany.id
            ? {
                ...c,
                name: editName,
                deviceLimit: editDeviceLimit,
                userLimit: editUserLimit,
                status: editActive ? "active" : "inactive",
              }
            : c
        )
      );
    } else {
      // Cria nova
      const newCompany: Company = {
        id: Date.now(),
        name: editName,
        deviceLimit: editDeviceLimit,
        userLimit: editUserLimit,
        deviceCount: 0,
        userCount: 1,
        status: editActive ? "active" : "inactive",
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      };
      setCompaniesList((prev) => [...prev, newCompany]);
    }

    setModalOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      {/* Header & Quick Action */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">Gestão de Empresas</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mt-0.5">
            Gerencie permissões, cotas e instâncias de clientes no Esfera v3.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button icon={<Plus size={16} />} onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal()}>
          Nova Empresa
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* KPI Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-500/15 border border-violet-500/20 text-violet-400">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{activeCompaniesCount}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Empresas Ativas</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/15 border border-blue-500/20 text-blue-400">
            <Smartphone size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{totalDevicesAllocated}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Dispositivos Alocados</p>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <Card padding="sm" className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400">
            <Users size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-primary">{totalUsersAllocated}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium">Usuários Totais</p>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Search Input */}
      <div className="relative max-w-md flex items-center">
        <Search size={17} className="absolute left-3.5 text-tertiary pointer-events-none z-10" />
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Buscar empresa por nome..."
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full input-with-icon-left"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Grid de Cards de Empresas */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
        {filteredCompanies.map((company) => {
          const devicePct = Math.round(((company.deviceCount || 0) / company.deviceLimit) * 100);
          return (
            <Card key={company.id} padding="md" hover className="flex flex-col justify-between">
              <div>
                {/* Header Card */}
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-violet-600/15 border border-violet-500/20 text-violet-400">
                      <Building2 size={22} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-primary leading-tight">{company.name}</h3>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary mt-0.5">Criada em {company.createdAt}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Badge variant={company.status === "active" ? "success" : "neutral"} dot>
                      {company.status === "active" ? "Ativa" : "Inativa"}
                    </Badge>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(company)}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-tertiary hover:text-violet-300 hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      title="Editar Empresa"
                    >
                      <Pencil size={15} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Cotas & Uso */}
                <div className="space-y-3 my-4">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-tertiary flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Smartphone size={12} className="text-violet-400" /> Dispositivos
                      </span>
                      <p className="font-bold text-primary text-xs mt-0.5">
                        {company.deviceCount || 0}<span className="text-tertiary font-normal">/{company.deviceLimit}</span>
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-tertiary flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Users size={12} className="text-emerald-400" /> Usuários
                      </span>
                      <p className="font-bold text-primary text-xs mt-0.5">
                        {company.userCount || 0}<span className="text-tertiary font-normal">/{company.userLimit}</span>
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Barra de Progresso de Ocupação */}
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-tertiary mb-1">
                      <span>Ocupação da Cota</span>
                      <span className="font-semibold text-secondary">{devicePct}%</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white/5 overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-500 transition-all duration-300"
                        style={{ width: `${devicePct}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Ações Inferiores */}
              <div className="pt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Button
                  variant="primary"
                  size="sm"
                  className="flex-1"
                  icon={<ArrowRight size={14} />}
                  onClick={() => handleEnterCompany(company.id)}
                >
                  Gerenciar Empresa
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  size="sm"
                  icon={<Pencil size={14} />}
                  onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(company)}
                >
                  Editar
                </Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Modal Editar / Criar Empresa */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={modalOpen}
        onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
        title={editingCompany ? `Editar Empresa: ${editingCompany.name}` : "Nova Empresa"}
        footer={
          <>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setModalOpen(false)}>
              Cancelar
            </Button>
            <Button onClick={handleSaveCompany}>Salvar Alterações</Button>
          </>
        }
      >
        <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Nome da Empresa</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={editName}
              onChange={(e) => setEditName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Ex: REALESS RM COBRANÇAS"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Limite de Dispositivos</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={editDeviceLimit}
                onChange={(e) => setEditDeviceLimit(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0)}
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Limite de Usuários</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={editUserLimit}
                onChange={(e) => setEditUserLimit(parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 0)}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
            <div>
              <span className="font-semibold text-primary block">Status da Empresa</span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">Ativar ou suspender acesso desta instância</span>
            </div>
            <Toggle checked={editActive} onChange={setEditActive} size="sm" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\DashboardTab.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Dashboard Tab
// Painel de indicadores com KPIs, atividade recente e status
// ============================================================

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { usePermissions } from "../hooks/usePermissions";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { MOCK_COMPANIES } from "../mock-data";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import {
  BarChart3,
  Smartphone,
  MessageSquare,
  Clock,
  AlertTriangle,
  RefreshCw,
  TrendingUp,
  TrendingDown,
  Activity,
  Shield,
  Zap,
} from "lucide-react";

interface KpiData {
  blocked_chips: number;
  config_changes_today: number;
  sent_today: number;
  pending_contacts: number;
}

interface DeviceStatus {
  online: number;
  offline: number;
  total: number;
}

const defaultKpis: KpiData = {
  blocked_chips: 0,
  config_changes_today: 0,
  sent_today: 0,
  pending_contacts: 0,
};

export default function DashboardTab() {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();
  const { canAccessDashboard } = usePermissions();
  const [kpis, setKpis] = useState<KpiData>(defaultKpis);
  const [deviceStatus, setDeviceStatus] = useState<DeviceStatus>({ online: 0, offline: 0, total: 0 });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [lastRefresh, setLastRefresh] = useState<Date>(new Date());

  const selectedCompany = MOCK_COMPANIES.find((c) => c.id === selectedCompanyId);

  const fetchDashboard = async () => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await invoke<KpiData>("get_dashboard_kpis", { companyId: selectedCompanyId });
      setKpis(data);
    } catch {
      // Fallback mock
      setKpis({
        blocked_chips: 3,
        config_changes_today: 12,
        sent_today: 147,
        pending_contacts: 203,
      });
    }
    try {
      const devices = await invoke<{ id: number; status: string }[]>("get_devices", { companyId: selectedCompanyId });
      const online = devices.filter((d) => d.status === "online").length;
      setDeviceStatus({ online, offline: devices.length - online, total: devices.length });
    } catch {
      setDeviceStatus({ online: 4, offline: 1, total: 5 });
    }
    setLoading(false);
    setLastRefresh(new Date());
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchDashboard();
  }, [selectedCompanyId]);

  if (!canAccessDashboard) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-96 gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20">
          <BarChart3 size={32} className="text-red-400" />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">Acesso Restrito</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-tertiary text-center max-w-sm">
          Você não tem permissão para acessar o dashboard de indicadores.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const kpiCards = [
    {
      label: "Mensagens Enviadas Hoje",
      value: kpis.sent_today,
      icon: <MessageSquare size={22} />,
      color: "from-emerald-600 to-green-500",
      shadowColor: "shadow-emerald-500/20",
      trend: "+12%",
      trendUp: true,
    },
    {
      label: "Contatos Pendentes",
      value: kpis.pending_contacts,
      icon: <Clock size={22} />,
      color: "from-amber-600 to-orange-500",
      shadowColor: "shadow-amber-500/20",
      trend: "-5%",
      trendUp: false,
    },
    {
      label: "Chips Bloqueados",
      value: kpis.blocked_chips,
      icon: <AlertTriangle size={22} />,
      color: "from-red-600 to-rose-500",
      shadowColor: "shadow-red-500/20",
      trend: "+2",
      trendUp: true,
    },
    {
      label: "Alterações Hoje",
      value: kpis.config_changes_today,
      icon: <Activity size={22} />,
      color: "from-violet-600 to-indigo-500",
      shadowColor: "shadow-violet-500/20",
      trend: "Normal",
      trendUp: true,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-primary tracking-tight">
            Dashboard
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-tertiary mt-1">
            {selectedCompany ? selectedCompany.name : "Visão geral do sistema"} — Indicadores em tempo real
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="text-[10px] text-tertiary font-mono">
            Atualizado: {lastRefresh.toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR")}
          </span>
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            size="sm"
            onClick={fetchDashboard}
            loading={loading}
          >
            <RefreshCw size={14} />
            Atualizar
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* KPI Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {kpiCards.map((kpi) => (
          <Card key={kpi.label} hover glow padding="md">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs text-tertiary font-medium uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                  {kpi.label}
                </p>
                <p className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-primary tracking-tight">
                  {loading ? (
                    <span className="inline-block w-12 h-8 bg-white/5 rounded animate-pulse" />
                  ) : (
                    kpi.value.toLocaleString("pt-BR")
                  )}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className={`flex items-center justify-center w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${kpi.color} ${kpi.shadowColor} shadow-lg`}>
                <span className="text-white">{kpi.icon}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-3">
              {kpi.trendUp ? (
                <TrendingUp size={12} className="text-emerald-400" />
              ) : (
                <TrendingDown size={12} className="text-red-400" />
              )}
              <span className={`text-xs font-semibold ${kpi.trendUp ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-red-400"}`}>
                {kpi.trend}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-tertiary">vs. ontem</span>
            </div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Status dos Dispositivos + Atividade Recente */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {/* Status dos Dispositivos */}
        <Card padding="md" className="lg:col-span-1">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-primary flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <Smartphone size={16} className="text-violet-400" />
            Status dos Dispositivos
          </h3>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {[1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                <div key={i} className="h-8 bg-white/5 rounded animate-pulse" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Barra de status */}
              <div className="flex gap-1 h-3 rounded-full overflow-hidden bg-white/5">
                {deviceStatus.total > 0 && (
                  <>
                    <div
                      className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-green-400 rounded-full"
                      style={{ width: `${(deviceStatus.online / deviceStatus.total) * 100}%` }}
                    />
                    <div
                      className="bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 to-rose-400 rounded-full"
                      style={{ width: `${(deviceStatus.offline / deviceStatus.total) * 100}%` }}
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2.5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
                    <span className="text-xs text-secondary">Online</span>
                  </div>
                  <Badge variant="success">{deviceStatus.online}</Badge>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-red-400" />
                    <span className="text-xs text-secondary">Offline</span>
                  </div>
                  <Badge variant="danger">{deviceStatus.offline}</Badge>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-border">
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary">Total</span>
                  <Badge variant="purple">{deviceStatus.total}</Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>

        {/* Atividade Recente */}
        <Card padding="md" className="lg:col-span-2">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-primary flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <Zap size={16} className="text-violet-400" />
            Atividade Recente
          </h3>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {[
              { time: "14:32", event: "Dispositivo Galaxy A54 conectado", type: "success" as const },
              { time: "14:28", event: "Campanha 'Cobrança Junior' iniciada — 45 contatos", type: "info" as const },
              { time: "14:15", event: "Chip +5511999887766 marcado como restrito 24h", type: "warning" as const },
              { time: "13:50", event: "Automação 'Follow-up Senior' executada — 12 mensagens", type: "info" as const },
              { time: "13:22", event: "Configurações da empresa REALESS atualizadas", type: "neutral" as const },
              { time: "12:45", event: "Chip +5511988776655 banido por wa-ban", type: "danger" as const },
            ].map((item, i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/[0.05] shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-tertiary">{item.time}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-xs text-primary">{item.event}</p>
                </div>
                <Badge
                  variant={
                    item.type === "success" ? "success" :
                    item.type === "danger" ? "danger" :
                    item.type === "warning" ? "warning" :
                    item.type === "info" ? "info" : "neutral"
                  }
                  className="shrink-0"
                >
                  {item.type === "success" ? "OK" :
                   item.type === "danger" ? "Ban" :
                   item.type === "warning" ? "Alert" :
                   item.type === "info" ? "Info" : "Cfg"}
                </Badge>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Telemetria de Hardware & Licenciamento C++ HPC */}
      <Card padding="md" glow>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 shadow-lg shadow-violet-500/20">
                <Shield size={20} className="text-white" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-primary">Telemetria & Licenciamento C++ HPC</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-tertiary">
                  Engine Nativa Windows · Monitoramento de Kernel e Barramento USB
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Badge variant="purple" className="gap-1">
              <Activity size={12} />
              C++ HPC Active
            </Badge>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-border">
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border">
              <p className="text-[10px] text-tertiary uppercase font-mono">Assinatura Hardware UUID (SHA-256)</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-violet-300 mt-1 truncate">ESFERA-HWID-8F9A-4B2C-1D3E</p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border">
              <p className="text-[10px] text-tertiary uppercase font-mono">Instruções CPU Utilizadas</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-emerald-300 mt-1">AVX2 + SSE4.2 + Multi-core</p>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border">
              <p className="text-[10px] text-tertiary uppercase font-mono">Taxa Média de Vazão</p>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 mt-1">12.4 msgs/min (Delay Gaussiano)</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\DevicesTab.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Hub de Gestão de Dispositivos ADB Celulares
// Exibe EXCLUSIVAMENTE celulares ativamente conectados/adicionados.
// Sem quadros vazios ou slots fictícios. Com suporte a setores,
// botão de configurações (⚙️), remoção (🗑️) e espelhamento (Scrcpy 60FPS).
// ============================================================

import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback, useMemo, memo } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import Modal from "../components/ui/Modal";
import type { Device, DeviceStatus } from "../types";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import {
  Smartphone,
  Wifi,
  BatteryFull,
  BatteryLow,
  BatteryMedium,
  BatteryWarning,
  Plus,
  RefreshCw,
  Search,
  Eye,
  Home,
  ArrowLeft,
  Power,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Trash2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Zap,
  Settings,
  Camera,
  Grid,
  Table,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

export type SectorType = "junior" | "senior" | "pa_fixa" | "pesquisa";

const statusConfig: Record<
  DeviceStatus,
  { variant: "success" | "danger" | "warning" | "neutral"; label: string; pulse: boolean }
> = {
  online: { variant: "success", label: "Online", pulse: true },
  offline: { variant: "danger", label: "Offline", pulse: false },
  connecting: { variant: "warning", label: "Conectando", pulse: true },
  error: { variant: "danger", label: "Erro", pulse: false },
};

export default function DevicesTab() {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [sectorTab, setSectorTab] = useState<string>("all");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>("all");
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"grid" | "table">("grid");

  // Lista APENAS de celulares conectados/adicionados pelo usuário
  const [devicesList, setDevicesList] = useState<Device[]>([]);

  // Modais de Conexão e Diagnóstico
  const [connectModalOpen, setConnectModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [diagnosticModalOpen, setDiagnosticModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [mirrorModalOpen, setMirrorModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedDeviceForMirror, setSelectedDeviceForMirror] = useState<Device | null>(null);

  // Modal de Configurações de Celular (Renomear, Otimizar, Mudar Setor)
  const [configModalOpen, setConfigModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedDeviceForConfig, setSelectedDeviceForConfig] = useState<Device | null>(null);

  // Conexão USB / IP / Pareamento State
  const [connectTab, setConnectTab] = useState<"usb" | "manual" | "pair" | "apk">("usb");
  const [connectIp, setConnectIp] = useState("");
  const [connectPort, setConnectPort] = useState("5555");
  const [pairIp, setPairIp] = useState("");
  const [pairPort, setPairPort] = useState("");
  const [pairCode, setPairCode] = useState("");
  const [connectPortInput, setConnectPortInput] = useState("5555");
  const [usbDevices, setUsbDevices] = useState<{ serial: string; state: string; model: string }[]>([]);
  const [loadingUsb, setLoadingUsb] = useState(false);
  const [linkingSerial, setLinkingSerial] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [linkingStatus, setLinkingStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [setupDeviceData, setSetupDeviceData] = useState<{ serial?: string; ip?: string; port?: number } | null>(null);
  const [deviceNumberInput, setDeviceNumberInput] = useState("1");
  const [deviceNameInput, setDeviceNameInput] = useState("Aparelho 1");
  const [sectorInput, setSectorInput] = useState("junior");

  const effectiveCompanyId = selectedCompanyId || 1;

  const prepareDeviceSetup = (data: { serial?: string; ip?: string; port?: number }) => {
    const nextNum = devicesList.length + 1;
    setDeviceNumberInput(nextNum.toString());
    setDeviceNameInput(`Aparelho ${nextNum}`);
    setSetupDeviceData(data);
  };

  // Busca dispositivos ADB nativos em tempo real do Tauri
  const refreshDevices = useCallback(() => {
    invoke<Device[]>("get_devices", { companyId: effectiveCompanyId })
      .then((data) => {
        setDevicesList(data || []);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.warn("Nenhum dispositivo ADB retornado via IPC:", err);
        setDevicesList([]);
      });
  }, [effectiveCompanyId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    refreshDevices();
    const interval = setInterval(refreshDevices, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [refreshDevices]);

  const fetchUsbDevices = async () => {
    setLoadingUsb(true);
    setLinkingStatus(null);
    try {
      const data = await invoke<any[]>("list_usb_devices");
      setUsbDevices(data || []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("Falha ao buscar dispositivos ADB nativos:", err);
      setUsbDevices([]);
    } finally {
      setLoadingUsb(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (connectModalOpen && connectTab === "usb") {
      fetchUsbDevices();
    }
  }, [connectModalOpen, connectTab]);

  const handleForceWirelessAdb = async (serial: string, deviceNumber: number, sector: string, deviceName: string) => {
    setLinkingSerial(serial);
    setLinkingStatus("⚡ Forçando ativação da porta 5555 TCP/IP e extraindo IP do celular via USB...");
    try {
      const result = await invoke<string>("force_enable_wireless_adb", {
        serial,
        companyId: effectiveCompanyId,
        deviceNumber,
        sector,
        deviceName: deviceName || `Aparelho ${deviceNumber}`,
      });
      setLinkingStatus(result);
      refreshDevices();
      setTimeout(() => {
        setConnectModalOpen(false);
        setLinkingSerial(null);
        setLinkingStatus(null);
        setSetupDeviceData(null);
      }, 2500);
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errMsg = typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao forçar depuração Wi-Fi.";
      setLinkingStatus(`❌ Erro: ${errMsg}`);
      setTimeout(() => setLinkingSerial(null), 5000);
    }
  };

  const handleConnectDevice = async (ipAddress: string, portNumber: number, deviceNumber: number, sector: string, deviceName: string) => {
    try {
      let finalIp = ipAddress.trim();
      let finalPort = portNumber;

      if (finalIp.includes(":")) {
        const parts = finalIp.split(":");
        finalIp = parts[0].trim();
        finalPort = parseInt(parts[1], 10) || portNumber || 5555;
      }

      await invoke("connect_adb_device", {
        ip: finalIp,
        port: finalPort,
        companyId: effectiveCompanyId,
        deviceNumber,
        sector,
        deviceName: deviceName || `Aparelho ${deviceNumber}`,
      });
      refreshDevices();
      setConnectModalOpen(false);
      setSetupDeviceData(null);
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errMsg = typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao conectar por IP.";
      setLinkingStatus(`❌ Erro: ${errMsg}`);
    }
  };

  const handlePairAndConnectDevice = async (
    ip: string,
    pPort: number,
    code: string,
    cPort: number,
    devNum: number,
    sec: string,
    devName: string
  ) => {
    setLinkingStatus("🔑 Enviando código de pareamento de 6 dígitos para o Android via Wi-Fi...");
    try {
      const result = await invoke<string>("pair_and_connect_adb_device", {
        ip,
        pairPort: pPort,
        pairCode: code,
        connectPort: cPort || 5555,
        companyId: effectiveCompanyId,
        deviceNumber: devNum,
        sector: sec,
        deviceName: devName || `Aparelho ${devNum}`,
      });
      setLinkingStatus(result);
      refreshDevices();
      setTimeout(() => {
        setConnectModalOpen(false);
        setLinkingStatus(null);
        setSetupDeviceData(null);
      }, 2500);
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errMsg = typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro no pareamento por código.";
      setLinkingStatus(`❌ Erro: ${errMsg}`);
    }
  };

  // Remover Celular
  const handleRemoveDevice = async (serial: string) => {
    try {
      await invoke("delete_device", { serial });
      setDevicesList((prev) => prev.filter((d) => d.serial !== serial));
      refreshDevices();
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Falha ao apagar dispositivo no SQLite:", e);
    }
  };

  // Filtra APENAS os celulares reais existentes com memoização HPC para 0ms de lag no React
  const filteredDevices = useMemo(() => {
    return devicesList
      .filter((device, _, arr) => {
        // Se for uma serial USB offline e o mesmo modelo já estiver online via Wi-Fi, descarta a duplicata USB
        if (device.status === "offline" && !device.serial.includes(":")) {
          const hasOnlineWireless = arr.some(
            (other) => other.model === device.model && other.status === "online" && other.serial.includes(":")
          );
          if (hasOnlineWireless) return false;
        }

        if (sectorTab !== "all" && device.sector !== sectorTab) return false;
        if (statusFilter !== "all" && device.status !== statusFilter) return false;

        if (searchQuery) {
          const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
          const matchesModel = device.model.toLowerCase().includes(q);
          const matchesBrand = device.brand.toLowerCase().includes(q);
          const matchesSerial = device.serial.toLowerCase().includes(q);
          const matchesIp = device.ip ? device.ip.toLowerCase().includes(q) : false;
          return matchesModel || matchesBrand || matchesSerial || matchesIp;
        }

        return true;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => {
        const nameA = a.name || `Aparelho ${a.deviceNumber}`;
        const nameB = b.name || `Aparelho ${b.deviceNumber}`;
        return nameA.localeCompare(nameB, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" });
      });
  }, [devicesList, sectorTab, statusFilter, searchQuery]);

  const onlineCount = devicesList.filter((d) => d.status === "online").length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in text-slate-100 font-sans pb-10">
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* BARRA SUPERIOR: FILTRO DE SETORES & BUSCA DE CELULARES */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row xl:items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-[#0d111c]/90 border border-slate-800/80 p-3 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md">
        {/* Tabs de Filtro de Setor */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar pb-1 xl:pb-0">
          {[
            { id: "all", label: "Todos os Setores" },
            { id: "junior", label: "Junior" },
            { id: "senior", label: "Senior" },
            { id: "pa_fixa", label: "PA FIXA" },
            { id: "pesquisa", label: "Pesquisa" },
          ].map((tab) => (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              onClick={() => setSectorTab(tab.id)}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                sectorTab === tab.id
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/40 border border-blue-400/30 scale-105"
                  : "bg-slate-900/80 text-slate-300 border border-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Busca e Ações Principais */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[240px] sm:min-w-[320px]">
            <Search size={15} className="absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 pointer-events-none" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Buscar celular por modelo, marca ou IP..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-[#121624] border border-slate-700/70 text-slate-100 rounded-full pl-9 pr-4 py-2 text-xs placeholder:text-slate-500 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20"
            />
          </div>

          <Button
            onClick={async () => {
              if (devicesList.length === 0) {
                alert("Nenhum celular detectado para instalação remota.");
                return;
              }
              let ok = 0;
              for (const dev of devicesList) {
                try {
                  await invoke("install_agent_apk", { serial: dev.serial });
                  ok++;
                } catch (e) {
                  console.warn("Erro ao instalar no dispositivo:", e);
                }
              }
              alert(`📲 Instalação remota concluída!\nO Agente APK foi enviado e ativado em ${ok} de ${devicesList.length} celulares com sucesso!`);
            }}
            icon={<Smartphone size={15} className="text-emerald-300" />}
            className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-500 text-white font-extrabold rounded-xl text-xs shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/30 cursor-pointer"
            title="Transfere e instala silenciosamente o app Agente Esfera v3 no celular via ADB"
          >
            📲 Auto-Instalar Agente APK
          </Button>

          <Button
            onClick={() => setConnectModalOpen(true)}
            icon={<Plus size={15} />}
            className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/30"
          >
            + Conectar Celular
          </Button>

          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={refreshDevices}
            icon={<RefreshCw size={14} className="text-emerald-400" />}
            className="bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
          >
            Sincronizar ADB
          </Button>

          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => setDiagnosticModalOpen(true)}
            icon={<AlertTriangle size={14} className="text-amber-400" />}
            className="bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs"
          >
            Diagnóstico ADB
          </Button>

          {/* Toggle Grade x Tabela */}
          <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-1 gap-1">
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode("grid")}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                viewMode === "grid" ? "bg-blue-600 text-white" : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
              title="Visão em Grade"
            >
              <Grid size={15} />
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode("table")}
              className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                viewMode === "table" ? "bg-blue-600 text-white" : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
              title="Visão em Tabela ADB"
            >
              <Table size={15} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* SEGUNDA BARRA: STATUS DA REDE ADB & ESTATÍSTICAS */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-[#0d111c]/60 border border-slate-800/50 p-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-slate-300">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold">
            <Smartphone size={15} className="text-blue-400" /> Total Celulares:{" "}
            <span className="text-white font-mono">{devicesList.length}</span>
          </span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold">
            <Wifi size={15} className="text-emerald-400" /> Online:{" "}
            <span className="text-emerald-300 font-mono">{onlineCount}</span>
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Filtros rápidos de Status */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider mr-1">Status:</span>
          {[
            { id: "all", label: "Todos" },
            { id: "online", label: "Online" },
            { id: "offline", label: "Offline" },
            { id: "connecting", label: "Conectando" },
          ].map((st) => (
            <button
              key={st.id}
              onClick={() => setStatusFilter(st.id)}
              className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold border transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                statusFilter === st.id
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-400/40"
                  : "bg-slate-900 text-slate-400 border-slate-800 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {st.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* CONTEÚDO PRINCIPAL: EXIBE APENAS CELULARES CONECTADOS */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {viewMode === "grid" ? (
        filteredDevices.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            {filteredDevices.map((device) => (
              <MobileDeviceCardItem
                key={device.serial}
                device={device}
                onRemove={() => handleRemoveDevice(device.serial)}
                onOpenMirror={() => {
                  setSelectedDeviceForMirror(device);
                  setMirrorModalOpen(true);
                }}
                onOpenConfig={() => {
                  setSelectedDeviceForConfig(device);
                  setConfigModalOpen(true);
                }}
                onForceWireless={() => {
                  handleForceWirelessAdb(
                    device.serial,
                    device.deviceNumber,
                    device.sector,
                    device.name || `Aparelho ${device.deviceNumber}`
                  );
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* ESTADO VAZIO LIMPO (SEM QUADROS DE SLOTS) */
          <div className="py-20 text-center bg-[#0d111c]/50 border border-dashed border-slate-800/80 rounded-2xl p-8 space-y-4 shadow-xl">
            <Smartphone size={48} className="mx-auto text-blue-500/40 animate-pulse" />
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-200">Nenhum celular conectado neste setor</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto">
                Conecte um aparelho Android via cabo USB ou Wi-Fi (ADB) para gerenciá-lo em tempo real.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-center gap-3 pt-2">
              <Button onClick={() => setConnectModalOpen(true)} icon={<Plus size={15} />}>
                + Conectar Celular
              </Button>
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={refreshDevices} icon={<RefreshCw size={14} />}>
                Sincronizar ADB
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )
      ) : (
        /* VISÃO EM TABELA ADB */
        <Card padding="none">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-border text-[11px] font-bold text-tertiary uppercase tracking-wider bg-white/[0.01]">
                  <th className="px-6 py-3.5">Modelo do Celular</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-3.5">IP / Serial ADB</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-3.5">Setor</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-3.5">Status Conexão</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-3.5">Bateria</th>
                  <th className="px-6 py-3.5 text-right">Controle Remoto</th>
