                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-border text-xs">
                {filteredDevices.map((device) => (
                  <tr key={device.serial} className="hover:bg-white/[0.02] transition-colors">
                    <td className="px-6 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/15 border border-blue-500/30 text-blue-400">
                          <Smartphone size={20} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                          <p className="font-bold text-primary">{device.model}</p>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">{device.brand}</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 font-mono text-secondary">{device.serial}</td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 uppercase font-bold text-blue-400">{device.sector}</td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4">
                      <Badge variant={statusConfig[device.status]?.variant || "neutral"}>
                        {statusConfig[device.status]?.label || device.status}
                      </Badge>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4">
                      <BatteryBar level={device.battery} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right space-x-2">
                      <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        icon={<Eye size={14} className="text-blue-400" />}
                        onClick={() => {
                          setSelectedDeviceForMirror(device);
                          setMirrorModalOpen(true);
                        }}
                      >
                        Espelhar Tela
                      </Button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* MODAIS DO SISTEMA */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}

      {/* Modal de Configurações do Celular (Renomear, Otimizar, Mudar Setor) */}
      {configModalOpen && selectedDeviceForConfig && (
        <DeviceSlotConfigModal
          isOpen={configModalOpen}
          onClose={() => {
            setConfigModalOpen(false);
            setSelectedDeviceForConfig(null);
          }}
          device={selectedDeviceForConfig}
          onSave={async (updatedDevice) => {
            try {
              await invoke("update_device", { device: updatedDevice });
            } catch (e) {
              console.warn("Erro ao salvar alteração no SQLite:", e);
            }
            setDevicesList((prev) =>
              prev.map((d) => (d.serial === updatedDevice.serial ? updatedDevice : d))
            );
            setConfigModalOpen(false);
            refreshDevices();
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* Modal Conectar Dispositivo (USB / Wi-Fi) */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={connectModalOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          setConnectModalOpen(false);
          setSetupDeviceData(null);
        }}
        title="Conectar Novo Celular Android (ADB)"
        size="lg"
        footer={
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center w-full">
            {setupDeviceData ? (
              <div className="flex gap-2 ml-auto">
                <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSetupDeviceData(null)} disabled={linkingSerial !== null}>
                  Voltar
                </Button>
                <Button
                  onClick={() => {
                    const devNum = parseInt(deviceNumberInput, 10) || 1;
                    if (setupDeviceData.serial) {
                      handleForceWirelessAdb(setupDeviceData.serial, devNum, sectorInput, deviceNameInput);
                    } else if (setupDeviceData.ip) {
                      handleConnectDevice(setupDeviceData.ip, setupDeviceData.port || 5555, devNum, sectorInput, deviceNameInput);
                    }
                  }}
                  disabled={linkingSerial !== null}
                  className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold"
                >
                  {linkingSerial ? "Forçando Wi-Fi ADB..." : "⚡ Forçar Wi-Fi ADB e Conectar"}
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                {connectTab === "usb" && (
                  <Button variant="secondary" icon={<RefreshCw size={13} />} onClick={fetchUsbDevices} disabled={loadingUsb}>
                    Detectar Novamente
                  </Button>
                )}
                <div className="flex gap-2 ml-auto">
                  <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setConnectModalOpen(false)}>
                    Cancelar
                  </Button>
                  {connectTab === "manual" && (
                    <Button
                      onClick={() => {
                        if (!connectIp) return;
                        prepareDeviceSetup({ ip: connectIp, port: parseInt(connectPort, 10) || 5555 });
                      }}
                    >
                      Avançar
                    </Button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        }
      >
        {setupDeviceData ? (
          <div className="space-y-4 text-xs animate-scale-in">
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-primary mb-1">Definir Nome, Slot e Setor do Celular</h4>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">
                Organize o nome e o setor de destino para este dispositivo antes de finalizar o pareamento.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Nome do Aparelho (Título Principal)</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={deviceNameInput}
                onChange={(e) => setDeviceNameInput(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ex: Aparelho 1, Aparelho 2"
                className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-bold placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-blue-500/40"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Número do Slot</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  max="100"
                  value={deviceNumberInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setDeviceNumberInput(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ex: 1"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-blue-500/40"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Setor do Aparelho</label>
                <select
                  value={sectorInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setSectorInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary focus:border-blue-500/40"
                >
                  <option value="junior">Junior</option>
                  <option value="senior">Senior</option>
                  <option value="pa_fixa">PA FIXA</option>
                  <option value="pesquisa">Pesquisa</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {linkingStatus && (
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-blue-600/10 border border-blue-500/20 text-blue-200 text-[11px] animate-scale-in">
                <p className="font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1 text-blue-300">
                  <RefreshCw size={12} className="animate-spin" /> Conectando ao Android via ADB...
                </p>
                <p className="font-mono">{linkingStatus}</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
            <div className="flex border-b border-border mb-4">
              <button
                onClick={() => setConnectTab("usb")}
                className={`flex-1 py-2 text-center font-semibold cursor-pointer border-b-2 transition-all ${
                  connectTab === "usb"
                    ? "border-blue-500 text-blue-400 bg-white/[0.01]"
                    : "border-transparent text-secondary hover:text-primary"
                }`}
              >
                🔌 Pareamento USB para Wi-Fi (Automático)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setConnectTab("manual")}
                className={`flex-1 py-2 text-center font-semibold cursor-pointer border-b-2 transition-all ${
                  connectTab === "manual"
                    ? "border-blue-500 text-blue-400 bg-white/[0.01]"
                    : "border-transparent text-secondary hover:text-primary"
                }`}
              >
                🌐 Conexão Direta por IP (Manual)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setConnectTab("pair")}
                className={`flex-1 py-2 text-center font-semibold cursor-pointer border-b-2 transition-all ${
                  connectTab === "pair"
                    ? "border-blue-500 text-blue-400 bg-white/[0.01]"
                    : "border-transparent text-secondary hover:text-primary"
                }`}
              >
                🔑 Parear Código (Android 11+)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setConnectTab("apk")}
                className={`flex-1 py-2 text-center font-semibold cursor-pointer border-b-2 transition-all ${
                  connectTab === "apk"
                    ? "border-blue-500 text-blue-400 bg-white/[0.01]"
                    : "border-transparent text-secondary hover:text-primary"
                }`}
              >
                📱 App Agente APK (No Celular)
              </button>
            </div>

            {connectTab === "usb" ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">
                  Dispositivos Android detectados no computador (USB e Wi-Fi). Selecione um aparelho para vincular ou forçar Wi-Fi ADB.
                </p>

                {loadingUsb ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8 text-center text-secondary font-semibold">
                    <RefreshCw size={24} className="animate-spin text-blue-400 mb-2" />
                    <span>Escaneando dispositivos ADB no computador...</span>
                  </div>
                ) : usbDevices.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-8 bg-white/[0.01] border border-dashed border-border rounded-xl">
                    <Smartphone size={32} className="mx-auto text-tertiary opacity-30 mb-2" />
                    <p className="font-semibold text-secondary">Nenhum celular detectado no ADB</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary mt-0.5 mb-3">Conecte o celular via cabo USB ou verifique se o Wi-Fi ADB está ativo.</p>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                    {usbDevices.map((dev) => (
                      <div key={dev.serial} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border">
                        <div>
                          <p className="font-semibold text-primary">{dev.model || "Aparelho Android"}</p>
                          <p className="text-[10px] text-tertiary font-mono">
                            {dev.serial.includes(":") ? "🟢 IP Wi-Fi: " : "🔌 Cabo USB: "}
                            {dev.serial} | Estado: {dev.state}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          onClick={() => {
                            if (dev.serial.includes(":")) {
                              const ipOnly = dev.serial.split(":")[0];
                              prepareDeviceSetup({ ip: ipOnly, port: 5555 });
                            } else {
                              prepareDeviceSetup({ serial: dev.serial });
                            }
                          }}
                        >
                          {dev.serial.includes(":") ? "Sincronizar" : "Vincular via Wi-Fi"}
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : connectTab === "manual" ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Endereço IP do Celular na Rede</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={connectIp}
                    onChange={(e) => setConnectIp(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Ex: 192.168.1.105 ou 192.168.1.82:9093"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Porta ADB (Padrão 5555)</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={connectPort}
                    onChange={(e) => setConnectPort(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="5555"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : connectTab === "pair" ? (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">
                  No celular, acesse Opções do Desenvolvedor ➔ Depuração sem Fio ➔ "Parear com código de pareamento". Digite abaixo o IP, Porta e o Código de 6 dígitos gerados no celular.
                </p>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">IP do Celular</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={pairIp}
                      onChange={(e) => setPairIp(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Ex: 192.168.1.82"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono text-xs"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Porta de Pareamento</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={pairPort}
                      onChange={(e) => setPairPort(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Ex: 37123"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono text-xs"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Código (6 Dígitos)</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={pairCode}
                      onChange={(e) => setPairCode(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Ex: 849201"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono text-xs tracking-widest text-center font-bold text-amber-300"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Porta Conexão/Depuração</label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={connectPortInput}
                      onChange={(e) => setConnectPortInput(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Ex: 9093 ou 5555"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-primary font-mono text-xs"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <Button
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (!pairIp || !pairPort || !pairCode) return;
                    handlePairAndConnectDevice(
                      pairIp,
                      parseInt(pairPort, 10) || 37123,
                      pairCode.trim(),
                      parseInt(connectPortInput, 10) || 5555,
                      1,
                      "junior",
                      "Aparelho Pareado"
                    );
                  }}
                  className="w-full mt-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-600 to-orange-600 hover:from-amber-500 hover:to-orange-500 text-white font-extrabold text-xs py-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-lg cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  <Zap size={14} className="text-yellow-200" />
                  <span>🔑 Enviar Código e Conectar sem Fio</span>
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-blue-950/40 border border-blue-500/20 space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-400 shrink-0">
                      <Smartphone size={28} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-white text-sm">App Agente Esfera v3 (.APK)</h4>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                        Instale o aplicativo Agente direto no celular Android para ativar o Wi-Fi ADB com 1 toque sem cabo USB.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="border-t border-blue-500/20 pt-3 space-y-2 text-slate-300">
                    <p className="font-bold text-white text-xs">Passo a Passo de Instalação no Celular:</p>
                    <ol className="list-decimal list-inside space-y-1.5 text-[11px]">
                      <li>Envie o arquivo <span className="font-mono text-blue-300">Esfera_Agent_ADB.apk</span> para o celular.</li>
                      <li>No celular, abra o arquivo e toque em <strong>Instalar</strong>.</li>
                      <li>Abra o aplicativo <strong>Esfera Agente</strong> e toque no botão <strong>⚡ Ativar Conexão Esfera Wi-Fi</strong>.</li>
                      <li>O aplicativo ativará a porta 5555 e conectará o celular ao Esfera v3 automaticamente no seu computador!</li>
                    </ol>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-2">
                    <a
                      href="/Esfera_Agent_ADB.apk"
                      download="Esfera_Agent_ADB.apk"
                      className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/30 flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <Zap size={14} className="text-amber-300" />
                      <span>Baixar APK do Agente (.APK)</span>
                    </a>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>

      {/* Modal de Espelhamento e Transmissão de Tela */}
      {selectedDeviceForMirror && (
        <ScreenMirrorModal
          isOpen={mirrorModalOpen}
          onClose={() => setMirrorModalOpen(false)}
          device={selectedDeviceForMirror}
        />
      )}

      {/* Modal de Diagnóstico de Hardware USB */}
      <AdbDiagnosticModal
        isOpen={diagnosticModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setDiagnosticModalOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// COMPONENTE: CARTÃO DE DISPOSITIVO CELULAR ANDROID
// ============================================================
const MobileDeviceCardItem = memo(function MobileDeviceCardItem({
  device,
  onRemove,
  onOpenMirror,
  onOpenConfig,
  onForceWireless,
}: {
  device: Device;
  onRemove: () => void;
  onOpenMirror: () => void;
  onOpenConfig: () => void;
  onForceWireless: () => void;
}) {
  const status = statusConfig[device.status as DeviceStatus] || statusConfig.offline;
  const displayName = device.name || `Aparelho ${device.deviceNumber}`;
  const isUsbOnly = !device.serial.includes(":");

  return (
    <div className="bg-[#0e121d]/90 border border-slate-800/90 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3 shadow-xl hover:border-slate-700/80 transition-all flex flex-col justify-between">
      {/* Top Header: Nome Principal (Aparelho 1, 2...) + Subtítulo (Modelo) + Setor */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1 pb-1 border-b border-slate-800/60">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
          <div className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400 shrink-0">
            <Smartphone size={18} />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            {/* TÍTULO PRINCIPAL (Ex: Aparelho 1, Aparelho 2) */}
            <span className="font-black text-sm text-white block truncate tracking-tight">{displayName}</span>
            {/* SUBTÍTULO / RÓTULO (Ex: moto g22) */}
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold block truncate">
              {device.model} {isUsbOnly && "(Cabo USB)"}
            </span>
            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-blue-950/80 text-blue-300 border border-blue-800/60 text-[9px] font-bold uppercase inline-block mt-0.5">
              {device.sector.replace("_", " ")}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
          <Badge variant={status.variant} dot pulse={status.pulse}>
            {status.label}
          </Badge>

          <button
            onClick={async () => {
              try {
                const msg = await invoke<string>("install_agent_apk", { serial: device.serial });
                alert(msg);
              } catch (err: any) {
                alert(typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao instalar APK.");
              }
            }}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-500/20 transition-all cursor-pointer ml-1"
            title="Instalar App Agente (.APK) no Celular via ADB"
          >
            <Smartphone size={14} />
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={onOpenConfig}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition-all cursor-pointer"
            title="Configurações do Celular (Renomear, Otimizar, Mudar Setor)"
          >
            <Settings size={14} />
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              if (confirm(`Deseja remover ${displayName}?`)) {
                onRemove();
              }
            }}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-500 hover:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/20 transition-all cursor-pointer"
            title="Remover Dispositivo"
          >
            <Trash2 size={13} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Connection IP / Serial */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono">
        <span className="text-slate-400">{isUsbOnly ? "Serial USB:" : "IP ADB:"}</span>
        <span className="text-blue-300 font-bold">{device.serial}</span>
      </div>

      {/* Battery Level */}
      <div className="space-y-1">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400">
          <span>Bateria:</span>
          <span className="font-mono text-slate-200">{device.battery}%</span>
        </div>
        <BatteryBar level={device.battery} />
      </div>

      {/* BOTÃO FORÇAR WI-FI ADB SE ESTIVER NO CABO USB */}
      {isUsbOnly ? (
        <button
          onClick={onForceWireless}
          className="w-full mt-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-600 to-orange-600 hover:from-amber-500 hover:to-orange-500 text-white font-extrabold text-[11px] py-2 px-3 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-600/30 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 animate-pulse"
        >
          <Zap size={14} className="text-yellow-200" />
          <span>⚡ Forçar Wi-Fi ADB (Ativar Sem Fio)</span>
        </button>
      ) : (
        /* Scrcpy Screen Mirror & Camera Buttons */
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5 mt-1">
          <button
            onClick={onOpenMirror}
            className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-blue-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-bold text-xs py-2 px-2 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/30 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
            title="Abrir painel de espelhamento e controle remoto"
          >
            <Zap size={13} className="text-amber-300" />
            <span>Espelhar Tela</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={async () => {
              try {
                const msg = await invoke<string>("launch_camera_stream", { serial: device.serial, facing: "back" });
                alert(msg);
              } catch (err: any) {
                alert(typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao abrir câmera.");
              }
            }}
            className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-purple-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white font-bold text-xs py-2 px-2 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-purple-600/30 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
            title="Abrir Transmissão de Câmera Ao Vivo (60 FPS)"
          >
            <Camera size={13} className="text-purple-200" />
            <span>📷 Câmera</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
});

// ============================================================
// MODAL: CONFIGURAÇÕES DE DISPOSITIVO (Renomear, Otimizar, Mudar Setor)
// ============================================================
function DeviceSlotConfigModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  device,
  onSave,
}: {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  device: Device;
  onSave: (device: Device) => void;
}) {
  const [nameInput, setNameInput] = useState(device.name || `Aparelho ${device.deviceNumber}`);
  const [modelInput, setModelInput] = useState(device.model || "");
  const [sectorInput, setSectorInput] = useState<SectorType>((device.sector as SectorType) || "junior");
  const [optimizing, setOptimizing] = useState(false);
  const [optStatus, setOptStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleOptimize = async () => {
    setOptimizing(true);
    setOptStatus("Limpando cache de memória RAM e otimizando processos ADB...");
    try {
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500));
      setOptStatus("✅ Memória RAM e conexões ADB otimizadas com sucesso!");
    } catch {
      setOptStatus("❌ Erro na otimização.");
    } finally {
      setOptimizing(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={`Configurações: ${nameInput || device.model}`}
      size="md"
      footer={
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center w-full">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancelar
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              onSave({
                ...device,
                name: nameInput || device.name || `Aparelho ${device.deviceNumber}`,
                model: modelInput || device.model,
                sector: sectorInput,
              });
            }}
          >
            Salvar Alterações
          </Button>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-4 text-xs font-sans">
        <div>
          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-200 mb-1">Nome do Aparelho (Título Principal)</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={nameInput}
            onChange={(e) => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex: Aparelho 1, Aparelho 2"
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-slate-100 font-bold"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-200 mb-1">Modelo / Rótulo de Hardware (Subtítulo)</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={modelInput}
            onChange={(e) => setModelInput(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Ex: moto g22, Samsung Galaxy A54"
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-slate-100 font-bold"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block font-bold text-slate-200 mb-1">Setor do Aparelho</label>
          <select
            value={sectorInput}
            onChange={(e) => setSectorInput(e.target.value as SectorType)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-slate-100 font-bold"
          >
            <option value="junior">Junior</option>
            <option value="senior">Senior</option>
            <option value="pa_fixa">PA FIXA</option>
            <option value="pesquisa">Pesquisa</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-950/30 border border-blue-500/20 space-y-2">
          <h5 className="font-bold text-blue-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Zap size={14} className="text-amber-400" /> Otimização de Memória & ADB
          </h5>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            Libere cache do sistema Android e otimize o barramento de rede ADB em tempo real.
          </p>
          <Button
            onClick={handleOptimize}
            disabled={optimizing}
            icon={<RefreshCw size={13} className={optimizing ? "animate-spin" : ""} />}
            className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white font-bold text-xs"
          >
            {optimizing ? "Otimizando..." : "Otimizar RAM & ADB"}
          </Button>

          {optStatus && <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-300 mt-1">{optStatus}</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

// ---- Auxiliar Bateria ----
function BatteryIcon({ level }: { level: number }) {
  if (level > 60) return <BatteryFull size={16} className="text-emerald-400" />;
  if (level > 30) return <BatteryMedium size={16} className="text-amber-400" />;
  if (level > 10) return <BatteryLow size={16} className="text-orange-400" />;
  return <BatteryWarning size={16} className="text-red-400 animate-pulse" />;
}

function BatteryBar({ level }: { level: number }) {
  let color = "from-emerald-500 to-emerald-400";
  if (level <= 10) color = "from-red-500 to-red-400";
  else if (level <= 30) color = "from-orange-500 to-amber-400";
  else if (level <= 60) color = "from-amber-500 to-yellow-400";

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-[130px]">
      <BatteryIcon level={level} />
      <div className="flex-1 h-2 rounded-full bg-white/5 overflow-hidden">
        <div
          className={`h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r ${color} transition-all duration-500`}
          style={{ width: `${level}%` }}
        />
      </div>
      <span className="text-xs font-mono font-medium text-secondary w-8 text-right">{level}%</span>
    </div>
  );
}

// ---- Modal de Espelhamento de Tela (Scrcpy / Web Canvas) ----
function ScreenMirrorModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  device,
}: {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  device: Device;
}) {
  const [screenSrc, setScreenSrc] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loadingFrame, setLoadingFrame] = useState(false);
  const [autoRefresh, setAutoRefresh] = useState(false);
  const [lastTouchPos, setLastTouchPos] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const imgRef = useRef<HTMLImageElement>(null);

  const fetchScreenFrame = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    setLoadingFrame(true);
    try {
      const src = await invoke<string>("capture_screen", { serial: device.serial });
      setScreenSrc(src);
    } catch {
      setScreenSrc(null);
    } finally {
      setLoadingFrame(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen, device.serial]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) {
      setScreenSrc(null);
      setAutoRefresh(false);
      return;
    }

    let intervalId: any = null;
    if (autoRefresh) {
      fetchScreenFrame();
      intervalId = setInterval(fetchScreenFrame, 2000);
    }

    return () => {
      if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);
    };
  }, [isOpen, autoRefresh, fetchScreenFrame]);

  const handleImageClick = async (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLImageElement>) => {
    if (!imgRef.current) return;
    const rect = imgRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clickX = e.clientX - rect.left;
    const clickY = e.clientY - rect.top;

    const normX = Math.round((clickX / rect.width) * 1080);
    const normY = Math.round((clickY / rect.height) * 2400);

    setLastTouchPos({ x: normX, y: normY });

    try {
      await invoke("send_adb_tap", { serial: device.serial, x: normX, y: normY });
      if (autoRefresh) fetchScreenFrame();
    } catch {
      console.log(`Toque simulado na posição ADB: X=${normX}, Y=${normY}`);
    }
  };

  const handleKeyevent = async (code: number) => {
    try {
      await invoke("send_adb_keyevent", { serial: device.serial, keycode: code });
      if (autoRefresh) fetchScreenFrame();
    } catch {
      console.log(`Evento de tecla simulado: Keycode ${code}`);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={`Controle Remoto & Espelhamento: ${device.model} (${device.serial})`}
      size="lg"
      footer={
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-blue-300">
            <Zap size={13} className="text-amber-400" /> Otimizado para alto desempenho via ADB
          </span>
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Fechar Painel
          </Button>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center space-y-4 font-sans">
        <div className="w-full space-y-3 p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 border border-border">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Sparkles size={14} className="text-blue-400" /> Transmissão de Tela & Ações ADB
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={async () => {
                  try {
                    const msg = await invoke<string>("launch_scrcpy_stream", { serial: device.serial });
                    alert(msg);
                  } catch (err: any) {
                    alert(typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao iniciar Scrcpy.");
                  }
                }}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-lg hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-500 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                ⚡ Scrcpy (60 FPS Tela)
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={async () => {
                  try {
                    const msg = await invoke<string>("inject_virtual_camera", { serial: device.serial, targetType: "screen" });
                    alert(msg);
                  } catch (err: any) {
                    alert(typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao injetar Câmera Virtual.");
                  }
                }}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-600 to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-extrabold shadow-lg hover:from-amber-500 hover:to-orange-500 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 animate-pulse"
                title="Substitui o sinal da câmera física do celular pela imagem do monitor do PC (Leitura de QR Code)"
              >
                🎯 Injetar Tela na Câmera
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={async () => {
                  try {
                    const msg = await invoke<string>("launch_camera_stream", { serial: device.serial, facing: "back" });
                    alert(msg);
                  } catch (err: any) {
                    alert(typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao iniciar Câmera Traseira.");
                  }
                }}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-lg hover:from-purple-500 hover:to-indigo-500 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5"
                title="Transmitir vídeo da câmera traseira em tempo real"
              >
                📷 Câmera Traseira
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={async () => {
                  try {
                    const msg = await invoke<string>("launch_camera_stream", { serial: device.serial, facing: "front" });
                    alert(msg);
                  } catch (err: any) {
                    alert(typeof err === "string" ? err : err?.message || "Erro ao iniciar Câmera Frontal.");
                  }
                }}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-600 to-rose-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-lg hover:from-pink-500 hover:to-rose-500 transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5"
                title="Transmitir vídeo da câmera frontal em tempo real"
              >
                🤳 Câmera Frontal
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  if (!autoRefresh) fetchScreenFrame();
                  setAutoRefresh(!autoRefresh);
                }}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-semibold transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                  autoRefresh
                    ? "bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30"
                    : "bg-blue-600/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30 hover:bg-blue-600/30"
                }`}
              >
                {autoRefresh ? "⏸️ Pausar Web Canvas" : "▶️ Iniciar Web Canvas"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1 border-t border-border/50">
            <button
              onClick={() => handleKeyevent(4)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-secondary hover:text-white border border-border text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <ArrowLeft size={14} /> Voltar
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleKeyevent(3)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-secondary hover:text-white border border-border text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <Home size={14} /> Início
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleKeyevent(187)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-secondary hover:text-white border border-border text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <Layers size={14} /> Recentes
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleKeyevent(26)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-red-500/10 hover:bg-red-500/20 text-red-300 border border-red-500/20 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1"
            >
              <Power size={14} /> Tela On/Off
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="relative w-64 h-[480px] rounded-[36px] bg-black border-4 border-slate-800 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 group select-none">
          <div className="w-16 h-4 bg-slate-900 rounded-full mb-1 z-20 flex items-center justify-center">
            <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-slate-800" />
          </div>

          <div className="relative w-full flex-1 rounded-[24px] overflow-hidden bg-slate-950 flex flex-col">
            {screenSrc ? (
              <img
                ref={imgRef}
                src={screenSrc}
                alt="Tela em Tempo Real"
                onClick={handleImageClick}
                className="w-full h-full object-contain cursor-pointer"
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-4 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-900 to-slate-950 text-center">
                <Smartphone size={36} className="text-blue-400 opacity-60 mb-3" />
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white mb-1">
                  {loadingFrame ? "Capturando Frame..." : autoRefresh ? "Buscando Tela..." : "Streaming Pausado"}
                </p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                  Clique no Scrcpy 60 FPS Nativo para espelhamento nativo fluido na sua área de trabalho.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {lastTouchPos && (
            <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 px-2 py-1 rounded-md bg-blue-600/80 text-[10px] text-white font-mono border border-blue-400/50 animate-fade-in z-30">
              Tap (X:{lastTouchPos.x}, Y:{lastTouchPos.y})
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

// ---- Modal de Diagnóstico USB/ADB ----
interface AdbDiagnosticResult {
  has_any_usb_device: boolean;
  has_android_usb_device: boolean;
  is_adb_process_running: boolean;
  detected_usb_count: number;
  diagnostic_msg: string;
}

function AdbDiagnosticModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
}: {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [diagnostic, setDiagnostic] = useState<AdbDiagnosticResult | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const runDiagnostic = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await invoke<AdbDiagnosticResult>("run_adb_diagnostic");
      setDiagnostic(result);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro no diagnóstico ADB:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) runDiagnostic();
  }, [isOpen]);

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Diagnóstico do Barramento USB & Servidor ADB"
      size="md"
      footer={
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
          Fechar Diagnóstico
        </Button>
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-4 text-xs text-secondary font-sans">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="py-8 text-center font-bold text-blue-400 animate-pulse">
            Escaneando portas USB do computador...
          </div>
        ) : (
          diagnostic && (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 border border-border">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                  {diagnostic.has_any_usb_device ? (
                    <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="text-emerald-400" />
                  ) : (
                    <XCircle size={16} className="text-red-400" />
                  )}
                  <p className="font-bold text-primary">Barramento USB Nativo</p>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">{diagnostic.diagnostic_msg}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\LoginPage.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Tela de Login Refatorada
// Design Glassmorphic Premium com proporções e espaçamentos perfeitos
// ============================================================

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { useTheme } from "../context/ThemeContext";
import { Zap, User, Lock, Eye, EyeOff, Loader2, Sun, Moon, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const { login, error } = useAuth();
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState(false);

  const fillCredentials = (u: string, p: string) => {
    setUsername(u);
    setPassword(p);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setShake(false);

    const success = await login(username, password);

    if (success) {
      navigate("/");
    } else {
      setShake(true);
      setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 600);
    }

    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center relative overflow-hidden bg-primary p-6">
      {/* Background Ambient Lighting */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden pointer-events-none">
        <div className="absolute -top-1/3 -left-1/3 w-2/3 h-2/3 bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-600/30 to-indigo-600/10 rounded-full blur-3xl animate-float" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-1/3 -right-1/3 w-2/3 h-2/3 bg-gradient-to-tl from-indigo-600/25 to-violet-600/10 rounded-full blur-3xl animate-float-delayed" />
      </div>

      {/* Theme Toggle Button */}
      <button
        onClick={toggleTheme}
        className="absolute top-6 right-6 p-3 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/10 transition-all z-20 cursor-pointer shadow-lg"
        title="Alternar Tema"
      >
        {isDark ? <Sun size={18} /> : <Moon size={18} />}
      </button>

      {/* Login Card Container */}
      <div className={`relative w-full max-w-md ${shake ? "animate-shake" : "animate-scale-in"}`}>
        <div className="rounded-3xl glass-panel shadow-2xl overflow-hidden border border-white/10 p-8 sm:p-10 space-y-6">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="text-center">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 shadow-xl shadow-violet-500/30 mb-4 animate-glow-pulse">
              <Zap size={32} className="text-white" />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-primary tracking-tight">Esfera v3</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-secondary mt-1 font-medium">
              Plataforma de Disparos e Gestão ADB
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            {error && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 text-xs font-medium animate-fade-in">
                <span>⚠️</span>
                <span>{error}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Input Usuário */}
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="login-username">Usuário de Acesso</label>
              <div className="relative flex items-center">
                <User size={18} className="absolute left-3.5 text-tertiary pointer-events-none z-10" />
                <input
                  id="login-username"
                  type="text"
                  value={username}
                  onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Digite seu usuário..."
                  className="input-with-icon-left"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Input Senha */}
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="login-password">Senha de Acesso</label>
              <div className="relative flex items-center">
                <Lock size={18} className="absolute left-3.5 text-tertiary pointer-events-none z-10" />
                <input
                  id="login-password"
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Digite sua senha..."
                  className="input-with-icon-left input-with-icon-right"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute right-3.5 text-tertiary hover:text-secondary transition-colors cursor-pointer z-10"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={18} /> : <Eye size={18} />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Submit Button */}
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || !username || !password}
              className="w-full h-12 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-violet-500 hover:to-indigo-500 shadow-lg shadow-violet-500/25 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-4"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" />
                  <span>Autenticando...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <span>Acessar Plataforma</span>
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          {/* Quick Credentials Widget */}
          <div className="pt-2">
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-tertiary uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">
                <Sparkles size={14} className="text-violet-400" />
                <span>Atalhos de Login Rápido</span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => fillCredentials("vicenzzo.master", "root123")}
                  className="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-violet-600/20 hover:bg-violet-600/30 text-xs font-semibold text-violet-300 border border-violet-500/30 transition-colors cursor-pointer text-center"
                >
                  Vicenzzo Master
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => fillCredentials("vicenzzo.mastronikolis", "Ope@2025")}
                  className="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600/20 hover:bg-indigo-600/30 text-xs font-semibold text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-500/30 transition-colors cursor-pointer text-center"
                >
                  Gestor Realess
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\LogsTab.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Logs Tab Avançado
// Console de logs com filtros avançados, exportação CSV e auto-refresh
// ============================================================

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import type { LogType, LogEntry } from "../types";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import {
  Search,
  Info,
  AlertTriangle,
  XCircle,
  CheckCircle,
  Trash2,
  Download,
  Terminal,
  RefreshCw,
  Smartphone,
  Megaphone,
  Bot,
  Shield,
  Settings,
  Clock,
} from "lucide-react";

const logTypeConfig: Record<
  LogType,
  { variant: "info" | "warning" | "danger" | "success"; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode; textColor: string }
> = {
  info: { variant: "info", label: "Info", icon: <Info size={14} />, textColor: "text-blue-400" },
  warning: { variant: "warning", label: "Aviso", icon: <AlertTriangle size={14} />, textColor: "text-amber-400" },
  error: { variant: "danger", label: "Erro", icon: <XCircle size={14} />, textColor: "text-red-400" },
  success: { variant: "success", label: "Sucesso", icon: <CheckCircle size={14} />, textColor: "text-emerald-400" },
  dispatch: { variant: "success", label: "Disparo", icon: <Megaphone size={14} />, textColor: "text-emerald-400" },
  ban: { variant: "danger", label: "Ban", icon: <Shield size={14} />, textColor: "text-red-400" },
  ai: { variant: "info", label: "IA", icon: <Bot size={14} />, textColor: "text-violet-400" },
  system: { variant: "info", label: "Sistema", icon: <Settings size={14} />, textColor: "text-slate-400" },
  device: { variant: "warning", label: "Device", icon: <Smartphone size={14} />, textColor: "text-amber-400" },
};

const ALL_LOG_TYPES: LogType[] = ["info", "warning", "error", "success", "dispatch", "ban", "ai", "system", "device"];

export default function LogsTab() {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState<LogType | "all">("all");
  const [sourceFilter, setSourceFilter] = useState("all");
  const [dateFrom, setDateFrom] = useState("");
  const [dateTo, setDateTo] = useState("");
  const [logsList, setLogsList] = useState<LogEntry[]>([]);
  const [autoRefresh, setAutoRefresh] = useState(false);
  const [lastRefresh, setLastRefresh] = useState(new Date());

  const fetchLogs = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    try {
      const data = await invoke<LogEntry[]>("get_logs", { companyId: selectedCompanyId });
      setLogsList(data);
    } catch {
      setLogsList([]);
    }
    setLastRefresh(new Date());
  }, [selectedCompanyId]);

  useEffect(() => { fetchLogs(); }, [fetchLogs]);

  // Auto-refresh every 5s
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!autoRefresh) return;
    const interval = setInterval(fetchLogs, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [autoRefresh, fetchLogs]);

  const filteredLogs = logsList
    .filter((l) => typeFilter === "all" || l.type === typeFilter)
    .filter((l) => sourceFilter === "all" || l.source === sourceFilter)
    .filter((l) => {
      if (!dateFrom && !dateTo) return true;
      const logDate = new Date(l.timestamp).getTime();
      if (dateFrom && logDate < new Date(dateFrom).getTime()) return false;
      if (dateTo && logDate > new Date(dateTo + "T23:59:59").getTime()) return false;
      return true;
    })
    .filter(
      (l) =>
        l.message.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
        l.source.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
        (l.deviceSerial && l.deviceSerial.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()))
    );

  const logCounts: Record<string, number> = {
    all: logsList.length,
  };
  ALL_LOG_TYPES.forEach((t) => {
    logCounts[t] = logsList.filter((l) => l.type === t).length;
  });

  const uniqueSources = [...new Set(logsList.map((l) => l.source))];

  const handleExportCSV = () => {
    const headers = ["Horário", "Nível", "Origem", "Dispositivo", "Mensagem"];
    const rows = filteredLogs.map((l) => [
      new Date(l.timestamp).toLocaleString("pt-BR"),
      logTypeConfig[l.type as LogType]?.label || l.type,
      l.source,
      l.deviceSerial || "",
      `"${l.message.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
    ]);
    const csv = [headers.join(","), ...rows.map((r) => r.join(","))].join("\n");
    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `esfera_logs_${new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]}.csv`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleClearLogs = async () => {
    try {
      await invoke("clear_logs", { companyId: selectedCompanyId });
    } catch { /* noop */ }
    setLogsList([]);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">Console de Logs</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mt-0.5">
            Histórico detalhado de eventos — disparos, bans, IA, dispositivos e sistema.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button
            variant={autoRefresh ? "primary" : "secondary"}
            size="sm"
            icon={<RefreshCw size={14} className={autoRefresh ? "animate-spin" : ""} />}
            onClick={() => setAutoRefresh(!autoRefresh)}
          >
            {autoRefresh ? "Auto-ON" : "Auto-OFF"}
          </Button>
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon={<Download size={14} />} onClick={handleExportCSV}>
            Exportar CSV
          </Button>
          <Button variant="danger" size="sm" icon={<Trash2 size={14} />} onClick={handleClearLogs}>
            Limpar
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats Row */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-5 lg:grid-cols-9 gap-2">
        {ALL_LOG_TYPES.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t}
            onClick={() => setTypeFilter(typeFilter === t ? "all" : t)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-medium transition-all cursor-pointer border ${
              typeFilter === t
                ? `${logTypeConfig[t].textColor} bg-white/[0.06] border-current`
                : "text-secondary bg-white/[0.02] border-border hover:bg-white/[0.04]"
            }`}
          >
            {logTypeConfig[t].icon}
            <span className="truncate">{logTypeConfig[t].label}</span>
            <span className="opacity-60 ml-auto">({logCounts[t] || 0})</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Advanced Filters */}
      <Card padding="sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-end gap-3">
          {/* Search */}
          <div className="relative w-full lg:w-80">
            <Search size={14} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-tertiary pointer-events-none" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Pesquisar mensagem, origem ou device..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-xs font-mono text-primary placeholder:text-tertiary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Source Filter */}
          <select
            value={sourceFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setSourceFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-xs text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
          >
            <option value="all">Todas as Origens</option>
            {uniqueSources.map((s) => (
              <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {/* Date From */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Clock size={12} className="text-tertiary" />
            <input
              type="date"
              value={dateFrom}
              onChange={(e) => setDateFrom(e.target.value)}
              className="px-2 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-xs text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
            />
            <span className="text-tertiary text-xs">até</span>
            <input
              type="date"
              value={dateTo}
              onChange={(e) => setDateTo(e.target.value)}
              className="px-2 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-border text-xs text-primary focus:border-violet-500/40 transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <span className="text-[10px] text-tertiary font-mono ml-auto">
            {filteredLogs.length} registros · {lastRefresh.toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR")}
          </span>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Logs Table */}
      <Card padding="none">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
          <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
            <thead className="sticky top-0 z-10 bg-card">
              <tr className="border-b border-border text-[10px] font-bold text-tertiary uppercase tracking-wider">
                <th className="px-4 py-3 w-40">Horário</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 w-20">Nível</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 w-28">Origem</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 w-28">Device</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">Mensagem</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-border text-xs font-mono">
              {filteredLogs.map((log, idx) => {
                const config = logTypeConfig[log.type as LogType] || logTypeConfig.info;
                return (
                  <tr key={log.id || idx} className="hover:bg-white/[0.02] transition-colors">
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-tertiary whitespace-nowrap">
                      {new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
                        day: "2-digit",
                        month: "2-digit",
                        hour: "2-digit",
                        minute: "2-digit",
                        second: "2-digit",
                      })}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                      <Badge variant={config.variant} dot className="text-[10px]">
                        {config.label}
                      </Badge>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-secondary font-sans font-medium">{log.source}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-tertiary font-sans">
                      {log.deviceSerial ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Smartphone size={10} />
                          {log.deviceSerial}
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        "—"
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className={`px-4 py-2.5 ${config.textColor}`}>{log.message}</td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>

          {filteredLogs.length === 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center text-tertiary">
              <Terminal size={40} className="mb-2 opacity-30" />
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-secondary">Nenhum log encontrado</p>
              <p className="text-xs">Ajuste os filtros ou aguarde novos eventos.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\PermissionsTab.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Painel de Permissões Granulares (Master Only)
// Gerencia permissões por usuário e por setor
// ============================================================

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import { MOCK_USERS } from "../mock-data";
import { Shield, Users, Save, RotateCcw, Check, Info } from "lucide-react";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import Badge from "../components/ui/Badge";
import Toggle from "../components/ui/Toggle";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import type { Permission, SectorPermission } from "../types";

const PERMISSION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  allow_all_tabs: "Acessar todas as abas",
  allow_edit_devices: "Editar dispositivos",
  allow_manage_users: "Gerenciar usuários",
  allow_dashboard: "Ver dashboard",
  is_active: "Conta ativa",
};

const PERMISSION_DESCRIPTIONS: Record<string, string> = {
  allow_edit_devices: "Pode adicionar, remover e gerenciar chips nos dispositivos",
  allow_manage_users: "Pode criar, editar e configurar outros usuários",
  allow_dashboard: "Pode acessar o painel de indicadores",
  allow_all_tabs: "Pode acessar todas as abas do sistema",
  is_active: "Status da conta do usuário",
};

const SECTOR_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  junior: "Junior",
  senior: "Senior",
  pesquisa: "Pesquisa",
  pa_fixa_1: "PA Fixa 1",
  pa_fixa_2: "PA Fixa 2",
  comercial: "Comercial",
};

const SECTOR_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  junior: "bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/30",
  senior: "bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300 border-purple-500/30",
  pesquisa: "bg-green-500/20 text-green-300 border-green-500/30",
  pa_fixa_1: "bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-300 border-yellow-500/30",
  pa_fixa_2: "bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-300 border-orange-500/30",
  comercial: "bg-pink-500/20 text-pink-300 border-pink-500/30",
};

const DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS: Permission[] = [
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_all_tabs", enabled: true },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_edit_devices", enabled: true },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_manage_users", enabled: false },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "allow_dashboard", enabled: true },
  { userId: 0, permissionKey: "is_active", enabled: true },
];

const DEFAULT_SECTOR_PERMISSIONS: SectorPermission[] = [
  { userId: 0, sector: "junior", canView: true, canConfigure: false },
  { userId: 0, sector: "senior", canView: true, canConfigure: false },
  { userId: 0, sector: "pesquisa", canView: true, canConfigure: false },
  { userId: 0, sector: "pa_fixa_1", canView: true, canConfigure: false },
  { userId: 0, sector: "pa_fixa_2", canView: true, canConfigure: false },
  { userId: 0, sector: "comercial", canView: true, canConfigure: false },
];

export default function PermissionsTab() {
  const { user: currentUser, hasPermission } = useAuth();
  const [selectedUserId, setSelectedUserId] = useState<string>("");
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<"user" | "sectors">("user");
  const [permissions, setPermissions] = useState<Permission[]>([]);
  const [sectorPermissions, setSectorPermissions] = useState<SectorPermission[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  const manageableUsers = MOCK_USERS.filter(
    (u) => u.id !== currentUser?.id && u.role !== "master"
  );

  const loadUserPermissions = useCallback(async (userId: number) => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [perms, sectorPerms] = await Promise.all([
        invoke<Permission[]>("get_user_permissions", { userId }),
        invoke<SectorPermission[]>("get_sector_permissions", { userId }),
      ]);
      setPermissions(perms.length > 0 ? perms : DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS.map((p) => ({ ...p, userId })));
      setSectorPermissions(sectorPerms.length > 0 ? sectorPerms : DEFAULT_SECTOR_PERMISSIONS.map((sp) => ({ ...sp, userId })));
    } catch {
      setPermissions(DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS.map((p) => ({ ...p, userId })));
      setSectorPermissions(DEFAULT_SECTOR_PERMISSIONS.map((sp) => ({ ...sp, userId })));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedUserId) {
      loadUserPermissions(Number(selectedUserId));
      setSaved(false);
    }
  }, [selectedUserId, loadUserPermissions]);

  const togglePermission = (key: string) => {
    setPermissions((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) => (p.permissionKey === key ? { ...p, enabled: !p.enabled } : p))
    );
    setSaved(false);
  };

  const toggleSectorPermission = (sector: string, type: "canView" | "canConfigure") => {
    setSectorPermissions((prev) =>
      prev.map((sp) => (sp.sector === sector ? { ...sp, [type]: !sp[type] } : sp))
    );
    setSaved(false);
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!selectedUserId) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      for (const perm of permissions) {
        await invoke("update_user_permission", {
          userId: Number(selectedUserId),
          permissionKey: perm.permissionKey,
          enabled: perm.enabled,
        });
      }
      for (const sp of sectorPermissions) {
        await invoke("update_sector_permission", {
          userId: Number(selectedUserId),
          sector: sp.sector,
          canView: sp.canView,
          canConfigure: sp.canConfigure,
        });
      }
      setSaved(true);
      setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 3000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Erro ao salvar permissões:", err);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    if (selectedUserId) loadUserPermissions(Number(selectedUserId));
  };

  const selectedUser = manageableUsers.find((u) => u.id === Number(selectedUserId));

  if (!hasPermission("allow_manage_users")) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-96 gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20">
          <Shield size={32} className="text-red-400" />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-primary">Acesso Restrito</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-tertiary text-center max-w-sm">
          Você não tem permissão para gerenciar permissões de outros usuários.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-primary tracking-tight">Gerenciar Permissões</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-tertiary mt-1">Controle granular de acesso por usuário e por setor</p>
        </div>
        <Badge variant="purple" className="gap-1">
          <Shield size={12} />
          Master
        </Badge>
      </div>

      {/* Seleção de Usuário */}
      <Card padding="md">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-primary flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <Users size={16} className="text-violet-400" />
          Selecionar Usuário
        </h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mb-3">Escolha o usuário para configurar permissões</p>
        <select
          value={selectedUserId}
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedUserId(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full max-w-md px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-border text-sm text-primary focus:outline-none focus:border-violet-500/50"
        >
          <option value="">Selecione um usuário...</option>
          {manageableUsers.map((u) => (
            <option key={u.id} value={String(u.id)}>
              {u.displayName} — {u.role === "gestor" ? "Gestor" : "Colaborador"}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Card>

      {/* Configurações */}
      {selectedUserId && (
        <>
          {/* Tabs */}
          <div className="flex gap-2 p-1 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-border w-fit">
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveTab("user")}
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                activeTab === "user"
                  ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600/20 to-indigo-600/20 text-violet-300 border border-violet-500/30"
                  : "text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/5"
              }`}
            >
              <Shield size={14} />
              Permissões de Usuário
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveTab("sectors")}
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                activeTab === "sectors"
                  ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-violet-600/20 to-indigo-600/20 text-violet-300 border border-violet-500/30"
                  : "text-secondary hover:text-primary hover:bg-white/5"
              }`}
            >
              <Users size={14} />
              Permissões por Setor
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Tab: Permissões de Usuário */}
          {activeTab === "user" && (
            <Card padding="md">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-primary mb-1">
                Permissões Gerais — {selectedUser?.displayName}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mb-4">Controle o acesso às funcionalidades do sistema</p>

              {loading ? (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-8">
                  <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-violet-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {permissions.map((perm) => (
                    <div
                      key={perm.permissionKey}
                      className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${perm.enabled ? "bg-green-400" : "bg-gray-500"}`} />
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-primary">
                            {PERMISSION_LABELS[perm.permissionKey] || perm.permissionKey}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-tertiary">
                            {PERMISSION_DESCRIPTIONS[perm.permissionKey] || ""}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <Toggle
                        checked={perm.enabled}
                        onChange={() => togglePermission(perm.permissionKey)}
                        disabled={perm.permissionKey === "is_active"}
                        size="sm"
                      />
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          )}

          {/* Tab: Permissões por Setor */}
          {activeTab === "sectors" && (
            <Card padding="md">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-primary mb-1">
                Permissões por Setor — {selectedUser?.displayName}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mb-4">Controle a visualização e configuração de cada setor</p>

              {loading ? (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-8">
                  <div className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-violet-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {/* Header */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_100px_100px] gap-2 px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-tertiary uppercase">
                    <span>Setor</span>
                    <span className="text-center">Visualizar</span>
                    <span className="text-center">Configurar</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="h-px bg-border" />

                  {sectorPermissions.map((sp) => (
                    <div
                      key={sp.sector}
                      className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_100px_100px] gap-2 items-center p-3 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border hover:bg-white/[0.04] transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Badge
                        variant="neutral"
                        className={`text-xs w-fit ${SECTOR_COLORS[sp.sector] || ""}`}
                      >
                        {SECTOR_LABELS[sp.sector] || sp.sector}
                      </Badge>
                      <div className="flex justify-center">
                        <Toggle
                          checked={sp.canView}
                          onChange={() => toggleSectorPermission(sp.sector, "canView")}
                          size="sm"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-center">
                        <Toggle
                          checked={sp.canConfigure}
                          onChange={() => toggleSectorPermission(sp.sector, "canConfigure")}
                          size="sm"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-3 rounded-xl bg-violet-500/10 border border-violet-500/20 mt-4">
                <Info size={16} className="text-violet-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                <p className="text-xs text-violet-300">
                  <strong>Visualizar</strong> permite ver os dispositivos e dados do setor.{" "}
                  <strong>Configurar</strong> permite editar configurações, campanhas e automações do setor.
                </p>
              </div>
            </Card>
          )}

          {/* Botões de Ação */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-border">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-tertiary">
              {saved ? (
                <>
                  <Check size={14} className="text-green-400" />
                  <span className="text-green-400 font-medium">Salvo com sucesso!</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <RotateCcw size={14} />
