                  <span>Alterações não salvas</span>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={handleReset}>
                <RotateCcw size={14} />
                Reverter
              </Button>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                onClick={handleSave}
                loading={saving}
              >
                <Save size={14} />
                {saving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar"}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src\pages\SettingsTab.tsx
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Settings Tab Completo
// Configurações da Empresa, IA, Dispatch, ADB e Segurança
// ============================================================

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";
import Card from "../components/ui/Card";
import Button from "../components/ui/Button";
import Toggle from "../components/ui/Toggle";
import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
import type { AiConfig } from "../types";
import {
  Save,
  Globe,
  Bell,
  Shield,
  Smartphone,
  Database,
  RefreshCw,
  Zap,
  RotateCcw,
  Brain,
} from "lucide-react";

export default function SettingsTab() {
  const { selectedCompanyId } = useAuth();

  // General
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState("REALESS RM COBRANÇAS");
  const [timezone, setTimezone] = useState("America/Sao_Paulo");

  // Notifications
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState(true);
  const [batteryAlert, setBatteryAlert] = useState(true);
  const [banAlert, setBanAlert] = useState(true);
  const [dispatchAlert, setDispatchAlert] = useState(false);

  // ADB
  const [autoReconnect, setAutoReconnect] = useState(true);
  const [pingInterval, setPingInterval] = useState(30);
  const [maxRetries, setMaxRetries] = useState(3);
  const [unlockPin, setUnlockPin] = useState("");

  // Dispatch
  const [delayMin, setDelayMin] = useState(30);
  const [delayMax, setDelayMax] = useState(90);
  const [humanizeDelay, setHumanizeDelay] = useState(true);
  const [batchSize, setBatchSize] = useState(5);
  const [retryOnFail, setRetryOnFail] = useState(true);
  const [maxRetriesDispatch, setMaxRetriesDispatch] = useState(2);

  // AI
  const [aiProvider, setAiProvider] = useState("ollama_local");
  const [aiEndpoint, setAiEndpoint] = useState("http://localhost:11434");
  const [aiModel, setAiModel] = useState("llama3.2");
  const [aiTemperature, setAiTemperature] = useState(0.7);
  const [aiApiKey, setAiApiKey] = useState("");
  const [aiSystemPrompt, setAiSystemPrompt] = useState("Você é um assistente de cobrança. Seja profissional e gentil.");
  const [aiTestResult, setAiTestResult] = useState("");

  // Security
  const [autoBackup, setAutoBackup] = useState(false);
  const [logRetention, setLogRetention] = useState("30");
  const [sessionTimeout, setSessionTimeout] = useState("60");

  // Load AI config
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    invoke<AiConfig>("get_ai_config", { companyId: selectedCompanyId })
      .then((cfg) => {
        if (cfg) {
          setAiProvider(cfg.provider || "ollama_local");
          setAiEndpoint(cfg.endpoint || "http://localhost:11434");
          setAiModel(cfg.model || "llama3.2");
          setAiTemperature(cfg.temperature || 0.7);
          setAiApiKey(cfg.apiKey || "");
          setAiSystemPrompt(cfg.systemPrompt || "");
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [selectedCompanyId]);

  const handleSaveAll = async () => {
    if (!selectedCompanyId) return;
    try {
      await invoke("save_ai_config", {
        companyId: selectedCompanyId,
        config: {
          provider: aiProvider,
          model: aiModel,
          temperature: aiTemperature,
          systemPrompt: aiSystemPrompt,
          apiKey: aiApiKey,
          endpoint: aiEndpoint,
        },
      });
    } catch { /* noop */ }
  };

  const handleTestAI = async () => {
    setAiTestResult("Testando...");
    try {
      const result = await invoke<string>("rewrite_message_ai", {
        message: "Olá {nome}, sua fatura está pendente.",
        contactName: "Maria",
      });
      setAiTestResult(result);
    } catch {
      setAiTestResult("[Mock] Bom dia Maria! Sua fatura está pendente. Att, Equipe REALESS");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in max-w-4xl">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">Configurações</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-tertiary mt-0.5">
            Preferências da empresa, IA, dispatch e segurança.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button icon={<Save size={16} />} onClick={handleSaveAll}>
          Salvar Tudo
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* === GERAL === */}
      <Card>
        <SectionHeader icon={<Globe size={18} />} color="violet" title="Informações Gerais" subtitle="Identificação da empresa e fuso horário" />
        <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Nome da Empresa</label>
            <input type="text" value={companyName} onChange={(e) => setCompanyName(e.target.value)} className="w-full input-setting" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Fuso Horário</label>
            <select value={timezone} onChange={(e) => setTimezone(e.target.value)} className="w-full input-setting">
              <option value="America/Sao_Paulo">América/São Paulo (GMT-3)</option>
              <option value="America/Manaus">América/Manaus (GMT-4)</option>
              <option value="America/Fortaleza">América/Fortaleza (GMT-3)</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* === NOTIFICAÇÕES === */}
      <Card>
        <SectionHeader icon={<Bell size={18} />} color="blue" title="Notificações & Alertas" subtitle="Alertas em tempo real do sistema" />
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <ToggleRow label="Alertas do Sistema" desc="Avisos quando dispositivo desconectar" checked={notifications} onChange={setNotifications} />
          <ToggleRow label="Alerta de Bateria Baixa" desc="Avisar se bateria < 20%" checked={batteryAlert} onChange={setBatteryAlert} />
          <ToggleRow label="Alerta de Ban Detectado" desc="Notificar quando chip for banido" checked={banAlert} onChange={setBanAlert} />
          <ToggleRow label="Alerta de Disparo" desc="Notificar ao completar lote de envio" checked={dispatchAlert} onChange={setDispatchAlert} />
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* === ADB & REDE === */}
      <Card>
        <SectionHeader icon={<Smartphone size={18} />} color="emerald" title="Conexão ADB & Redes" subtitle="Parâmetros de comunicação wireless" />
        <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
          <ToggleRow label="Reconexão Automática" desc="Tentar reconectar aparelhos offline" checked={autoReconnect} onChange={setAutoReconnect} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Intervalo de Ping (seg)</label>
              <input type="number" value={pingInterval} onChange={(e) => setPingInterval(parseInt(e.target.value) || 30)} min={5} max={300} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Max Tentativas (device)</label>
              <input type="number" value={maxRetries} onChange={(e) => setMaxRetries(parseInt(e.target.value) || 3)} min={1} max={10} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">PIN de Desbloqueio (opcional)</label>
            <input type="password" value={unlockPin} onChange={(e) => setUnlockPin(e.target.value)} placeholder="Ex: 1234" className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* === DISPATCH === */}
      <Card>
        <SectionHeader icon={<Zap size={18} />} color="amber" title="Motor de Disparos" subtitle="Delays, retry e humanização dos envios" />
        <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Delay Mínimo (seg)</label>
              <input type="number" value={delayMin} onChange={(e) => setDelayMin(parseInt(e.target.value) || 30)} min={5} max={300} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Delay Máximo (seg)</label>
              <input type="number" value={delayMax} onChange={(e) => setDelayMax(parseInt(e.target.value) || 90)} min={10} max={600} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Tamanho do Lote (contatos por vez)</label>
            <input type="number" value={batchSize} onChange={(e) => setBatchSize(parseInt(e.target.value) || 5)} min={1} max={50} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
          </div>
          <ToggleRow label="Humanização de Delay" desc="Usar distribuição Gaussiana ao invés de delay fixo" checked={humanizeDelay} onChange={setHumanizeDelay} />
          <ToggleRow label="Retry em Falha" desc="Tentar novamente contato que falhou" checked={retryOnFail} onChange={setRetryOnFail} />
          {retryOnFail && (
            <div className="pl-4 border-l-2 border-amber-500/30">
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Máximo de Retries por Contato</label>
              <input type="number" value={maxRetriesDispatch} onChange={(e) => setMaxRetriesDispatch(parseInt(e.target.value) || 2)} min={1} max={5} className="w-32 input-setting font-mono" />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* === IA === */}
      <Card>
        <SectionHeader icon={<Brain size={18} />} color="violet" title="Configuração de IA" subtitle="Provedor, modelo e parâmetros de geração" />
        <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Provedor</label>
            <select value={aiProvider} onChange={(e) => setAiProvider(e.target.value)} className="w-full input-setting">
              <option value="ollama_local">Ollama Local</option>
              <option value="ollama_cloud">Ollama Cloud</option>
              <option value="openai">OpenAI (GPT)</option>
              <option value="anthropic">Anthropic (Claude)</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Endpoint</label>
            <input type="text" value={aiEndpoint} onChange={(e) => setAiEndpoint(e.target.value)} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Modelo</label>
              <input type="text" value={aiModel} onChange={(e) => setAiModel(e.target.value)} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Temperatura ({aiTemperature})</label>
              <input type="range" min="0" max="1" step="0.1" value={aiTemperature} onChange={(e) => setAiTemperature(parseFloat(e.target.value))} className="w-full mt-2 accent-violet-500" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">API Key (opcional para local)</label>
            <input type="password" value={aiApiKey} onChange={(e) => setAiApiKey(e.target.value)} placeholder="sk-..." className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">System Prompt</label>
            <textarea value={aiSystemPrompt} onChange={(e) => setAiSystemPrompt(e.target.value)} rows={3} className="w-full input-setting resize-none" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-1">
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" icon={<RotateCcw size={13} />} onClick={handleTestAI}>
              Testar IA
            </Button>
            {aiTestResult && (
              <div className="flex-1 p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-violet-600/10 border border-violet-500/20 text-violet-200 text-xs font-mono">
                {aiTestResult}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* === SEGURANÇA === */}
      <Card>
        <SectionHeader icon={<Shield size={18} />} color="amber" title="Segurança & Retenção" subtitle="Backup, retenção de logs e sessão" />

          <ToggleRow label="Backup Automático Diário" desc="Snapshot local de campanhas e contatos" checked={autoBackup} onChange={setAutoBackup} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">

              <select value={logRetention} onChange={(e) => setLogRetention(e.target.value)} className="w-full input-setting">
                <option value="7">7 dias</option>
                <option value="15">15 dias</option>
                <option value="30">30 dias (Recomendado)</option>
                <option value="60">60 dias</option>
                <option value="90">90 dias</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block font-semibold text-secondary mb-1">Timeout de Sessão (min)</label>
              <input type="number" value={sessionTimeout} onChange={(e) => setSessionTimeout(e.target.value)} min={15} max={480} className="w-full input-setting font-mono" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2">
            <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" icon={<Database size={14} />}>
              Exportar Snapshot
            </Button>
            <Button variant="danger" size="sm" icon={<RefreshCw size={14} />}>
              Restaurar Padrão
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ---- Reusable Components ----
function SectionHeader({
  icon,
  color,
  title,
  subtitle,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  color: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
}) {
  const colorMap: Record<string, string> = {
    violet: "bg-violet-600/20 border-violet-500/30 text-violet-400",
    blue: "bg-blue-600/20 border-blue-500/30 text-blue-400",
    emerald: "bg-emerald-600/20 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-400",
    amber: "bg-amber-600/20 border-amber-500/30 text-amber-400",
    red: "bg-red-600/20 border-red-500/30 text-red-400",
  };
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-5 border-b border-border pb-4">
      <div className={`flex items-center justify-center w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border ${colorMap[color] || colorMap.violet}`}>
        {icon}
      </div>
      <div>
        <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-primary">{title}</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-tertiary">{subtitle}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ToggleRow({
  label,
  desc,
  checked,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  desc: string;
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (v: boolean) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-1">
      <div>
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary">{label}</p>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-tertiary">{desc}</p>
      </div>
      <Toggle checked={checked} onChange={onChange} />
    </div>
  );
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\adb_manager.rs
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Gerenciador ADB Nativo (adb_manager.rs)
// Comunicação nativa com o Android Debug Bridge CLI
// ============================================================

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct AdbDeviceInfo {
    pub serial: String,
    pub state: String,
    pub model: String,
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EsferaHardwareCaps {
    pub supports_avx2: bool,
    pub supports_avx512: bool,
    pub supports_sse42: bool,
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct EsferaAdbDiagnostic {
    pub has_any_usb_device: bool,
    pub has_android_usb_device: bool,
    pub is_adb_process_running: bool,
    pub detected_usb_count: i32,
    pub diagnostic_msg: [u8; 256],
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EsferaAdbDiagnosticResult {
    pub has_any_usb_device: bool,
    pub has_android_usb_device: bool,
    pub is_adb_process_running: bool,
    pub detected_usb_count: i32,
    pub diagnostic_msg: String,
}

extern "C" {
    pub fn esfera_hpc_detect_hardware(caps: *mut EsferaHardwareCaps);
    pub fn esfera_hpc_run_adb_diagnostic(diag: *mut EsferaAdbDiagnostic);
    pub fn esfera_hpc_sanitize_png_buffer(
        input_ptr: *const u8,
        input_len: usize,
        output_ptr: *mut u8,
        max_out_len: usize,
        out_written: *mut usize,
    ) -> i32;
    pub fn esfera_hpc_calculate_gaussian_delay(min_ms: i32, max_ms: i32) -> i32;
    pub fn esfera_hpc_generate_hardware_uuid(out_uuid_buf: *mut u8, max_len: usize);
    pub fn esfera_hpc_generate_bezier_points(
        start_x: i32,
        start_y: i32,
        end_x: i32,
        end_y: i32,
        out_pts_x: *mut i32,
        out_pts_y: *mut i32,
        max_points: i32,
        out_count: *mut i32,
    ) -> i32;
}

pub struct AdbManager;

impl AdbManager {
    /// Detecta capacidades da CPU via Engine C++ HPC nativa
    pub fn get_hardware_capabilities() -> EsferaHardwareCaps {
        let mut caps = EsferaHardwareCaps {
            supports_avx2: false,
            supports_avx512: false,
            supports_sse42: false,
        };
        unsafe {
            esfera_hpc_detect_hardware(&mut caps);
        }
        caps
    }

    /// Calcula atraso gaussiano imprevisível em C++ HPC
    pub fn calculate_gaussian_delay(min_ms: i32, max_ms: i32) -> i32 {
        unsafe { esfera_hpc_calculate_gaussian_delay(min_ms, max_ms) }
    }

    /// Obtém UUID de Licenciamento de Hardware vinculado à CPU e Placa-mãe em C++
    pub fn get_hardware_uuid() -> String {
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; 128];
        unsafe {
            esfera_hpc_generate_hardware_uuid(buf.as_mut_ptr(), buf.len());
        }
        let len = buf.iter().position(|&c| c == 0).unwrap_or(128);
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf[..len]).into_owned()
    }

    /// Varredura multithread de sub-rede na porta 5555
    pub fn scan_subnet(subnet_prefix: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        use std::net::TcpStream;
        use std::time::Duration;
        use std::sync::Mutex;
        use std::sync::Arc;

        let active_ips = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
        let mut handles = Vec::new();
        let prefix = if subnet_prefix.is_empty() { "192.168.1" } else { subnet_prefix.trim_end_matches('.') };

        for i in 1..=254 {
            let ip = format!("{}.{}", prefix, i);
            let active_ips_clone = Arc::clone(&active_ips);
            let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || {
                let addr = format!("{}:5555", ip);
                if let Ok(socket_addr) = addr.parse() {
                    if TcpStream::connect_timeout(&socket_addr, Duration::from_millis(150)).is_ok() {
                        if let Ok(mut ips) = active_ips_clone.lock() {
                            ips.push(ip);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
            handles.push(handle);
        }

        for handle in handles {
            let _ = handle.join();
        }

        let lock = active_ips.lock().unwrap();
        lock.clone()
    }

    /// Injeta scripts e propriedades persistentes no Android para proibir o desligamento do Wi-Fi e ADB
    pub fn deploy_keepalive(serial: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let commands = vec![
            "setprop persist.adb.tcp.port 5555",
            "settings put global wifi_sleep_policy 2",
            "settings put global stay_on_while_plugged_in 7",
            "settings put global low_power 0",
            "settings put global low_power_trigger_level 0",
            "settings put global wifi_scan_always_enabled 1",
            "settings put global adb_allowed_connection_time 0",
            "settings put global tcp_keepalive_interval 15",
            "dumpsys deviceidle disable",
            "dumpsys deviceidle whitelist +com.android.shell",
            "dumpsys deviceidle whitelist +com.google.android.gms",
        ];
        for cmd in commands {
            let _ = Command::new("adb")
                .args(["-s", serial, "shell", cmd])
                .output();
        }
        Ok("Injeção Keep-Alive ADB persistente aplicada no celular com sucesso!".to_string())
    }

    /// Executa o diagnóstico avançado de hardware/processos USB & ADB
    pub fn run_diagnostic() -> EsferaAdbDiagnosticResult {
        let mut diag = EsferaAdbDiagnostic {
            has_any_usb_device: false,
            has_android_usb_device: false,
            is_adb_process_running: false,
            detected_usb_count: 0,
            diagnostic_msg: [0u8; 256],
        };
        unsafe {
            esfera_hpc_run_adb_diagnostic(&mut diag);
        }
        
        let len = diag.diagnostic_msg.iter().position(|&c| c == 0).unwrap_or(256);
        let mut msg = String::from_utf8_lossy(&diag.diagnostic_msg[..len]).into_owned();

        // Checagem em tempo real no Windows PnP para falha física no cabo USB / descritor
        if let Ok(output) = Command::new("powershell")
            .args(["-Command", "Get-PnpDevice -PresentOnly | Where-Object { $_.Status -eq 'Error' } | Select-Object -ExpandProperty FriendlyName"])
            .output()
        {
            let pnp_err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
            if pnp_err.contains("Descritor") || pnp_err.contains("Desconhecido") {
                msg = "⚠️ ERRO DE HARDWARE USB NO WINDOWS: O Windows identificou 'Dispositivo USB Desconhecido (Falha na Solicitação de Descritor de Dispositivo)'. O cabo USB atual está fornecendo apenas energia/carregamento, mas falhou ao transmitir os dados de depuração. Por favor: 1) Troque o cabo USB; 2) Conecte diretamente em uma porta USB 3.0 traseira da placa-mãe; 3) Altere no celular para 'Transferência de Arquivos (MTP)'.".to_string();
            }
        }

        EsferaAdbDiagnosticResult {
            has_any_usb_device: diag.has_any_usb_device,
            has_android_usb_device: diag.has_android_usb_device,
            is_adb_process_running: diag.is_adb_process_running,
            detected_usb_count: diag.detected_usb_count,
            diagnostic_msg: msg,
        }
    }

    /// Reinicia o servidor ADB (kill-server & start-server)
    pub fn restart_server() -> Result<String, String> {
        let _ = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("kill-server")
            .output();

        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(1000));

        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("start-server")
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao iniciar servidor ADB: {}", e))?;

        if output.status.success() {
            Ok("Servidor ADB reiniciado com sucesso!".to_string())
        } else {
            let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
            Err(format!("Falha ao iniciar servidor ADB: {}", stderr.trim()))
        }
    }

    /// Executa `adb devices -l` e retorna a lista de dispositivos físicos/wireless conectados
    pub fn list_devices() -> Result<Vec<AdbDeviceInfo>, String> {
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("devices")
            .arg("-l")
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao executar comando adb: {}", e))?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            return Err("Comando 'adb devices' retornou erro.".to_string());
        }

        let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        let mut devices = Vec::new();

        for line in stdout.lines() {
            let line = line.trim();
            if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with("List of devices") || line.starts_with('*') {
                continue;
            }

            let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
            if parts.len() >= 2 {
                let serial = parts[0].to_string();
                let state = parts[1].to_string();

                let mut model = "Android Device".to_string();
                for part in &parts[2..] {
                    if part.starts_with("model:") {
                        model = part.replace("model:", "").replace('_', " ");
                    }
                }

                devices.push(AdbDeviceInfo {
                    serial,
                    state,
                    model,
                });
            }
        }

        Ok(devices)
    }

    /// Obtém o endereço IP do wlan0 do dispositivo para conexão Wireless
    pub fn get_device_wifi_ip(serial: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        // Método 1: getprop dhcp.wlan0.ipaddress
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("getprop")
            .arg("dhcp.wlan0.ipaddress")
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        
        let ip = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim().to_string();
        if !ip.is_empty() && ip.contains('.') {
            return Ok(ip);
        }

        // Método 2: ip route
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("ip")
            .arg("route")
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        let route_out = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        for line in route_out.lines() {
            if line.contains("wlan0") && line.contains("src") {
                let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
                if let Some(src_idx) = parts.iter().position(|&x| x == "src") {
                    if src_idx + 1 < parts.len() {
                        let parsed_ip = parts[src_idx + 1].trim().to_string();
                        if parsed_ip.contains('.') {
                            return Ok(parsed_ip);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Método 3: ip addr show wlan0
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("ip")
            .arg("addr")
            .arg("show")
            .arg("wlan0")
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

        let addr_out = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        for line in addr_out.lines() {
            if line.trim().starts_with("inet ") {
                let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split_whitespace().collect();
                if parts.len() > 1 {
                    let ip_mask = parts[1];
                    let ip_only: Vec<&str> = ip_mask.split('/').collect();
                    if !ip_only.is_empty() {
                        let parsed_ip = ip_only[0].trim().to_string();
                        if parsed_ip.contains('.') {
                            return Ok(parsed_ip);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Método 4: ifconfig wlan0
        if let Ok(output) = Command::new("adb").args(["-s", serial, "shell", "ifconfig", "wlan0"]).output() {
            let out = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
            for line in out.lines() {
                if line.contains("inet addr:") {
                    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split("inet addr:").collect();
                    if parts.len() > 1 {
                        let ip = parts[1].split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_string();
                        if ip.contains('.') {
                            return Ok(ip);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Método 5: dumpsys wifi
        if let Ok(output) = Command::new("adb").args(["-s", serial, "shell", "dumpsys", "wifi"]).output() {
            let out = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
            for line in out.lines() {
                if line.contains("ip_address=") {
                    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split("ip_address=").collect();
                    if parts.len() > 1 {
                        let ip = parts[1].split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_string();
                        if ip.contains('.') && !ip.starts_with("0.0.0.0") {
                            return Ok(ip);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Err(format!("Não foi possível detectar o IP Wi-Fi do celular '{}'. Verifique se o aparelho está conectado à rede Wi-Fi.", serial))
    }

    /// Habilita porta TCP no dispositivo
    pub fn enable_tcpip(serial: &str, port: i32) -> Result<(), String> {
        let _ = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("tcpip")
            .arg(port.to_string())
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao colocar em modo TCP/IP: {}", e))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Conecta a um dispositivo via IP:Porta usando `adb connect ip:port`
    pub fn connect_device(ip: &str, port: i32) -> Result<String, String> {
        let target = format!("{}:{}", ip, port);
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("connect")
            .arg(&target)
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao executar 'adb connect': {}", e))?;

        let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
        let combined = format!("{} {}", stdout, stderr);
        let lower = combined.to_lowercase();

        if lower.contains("connected to") || lower.contains("already connected") {
            Ok(format!("Dispositivo {} conectado com sucesso!", target))
        } else {
            Err(format!("Não foi possível conectar a {}: {}", target, combined.trim()))
        }
    }

    /// Executa `adb pair <ip>:<port> <code>` para parear celular Android 11+ via Wi-Fi sem cabo
    pub fn pair_device(ip: &str, port: u16, code: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let target = format!("{}:{}", ip, port);
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("pair")
            .arg(&target)
            .arg(code)
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao executar 'adb pair': {}", e))?;

        let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string();
        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
        let combined = format!("{} {}", stdout, stderr);
        let lower = combined.to_lowercase();

        if lower.contains("successfully paired") || lower.contains("success") || output.status.success() {
            Ok(format!("Celular {} pareado com sucesso com o código {}!", target, code))
        } else {
            Err(format!("Falha no pareamento em {}: {}", target, combined.trim()))
        }
    }

    /// Desconecta de um dispositivo via `adb disconnect serial`
    pub fn disconnect_device(serial: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("disconnect")
            .arg(serial)
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao executar 'adb disconnect': {}", e))?;

        let result = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string();
        Ok(result)
    }

    /// Obtém o nível de bateria de um celular via `adb -s <serial> shell dumpsys battery`
    pub fn get_battery_level(serial: &str) -> Result<i32, String> {
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("dumpsys")
            .arg("battery")
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao consultar bateria via ADB: {}", e))?;

        let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
        for line in stdout.lines() {
            let line = line.trim();
            if line.starts_with("level:") {
                let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split(':').collect();
                if parts.len() == 2 {
                    if let Ok(level) = parts[1].trim().parse::<i32>() {
                        return Ok(level);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(85) // Valor padrão de fallback se não puder ler
    }

    /// Captura a tela do dispositivo via `adb -s <serial> exec-out screencap -p` e aplica sanitização nativa C++ HPC
    pub fn capture_screen(serial: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .args(["-s", serial, "exec-out", "screencap", "-p"])
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao executar screencap ADB: {}", e))?;

        if output.status.success() && !output.stdout.is_empty() {
            let raw_bytes = &output.stdout;
            let mut sanitized = vec![0u8; raw_bytes.len()];
            let mut out_written: usize = 0;

            let status = unsafe {
                esfera_hpc_sanitize_png_buffer(
                    raw_bytes.as_ptr(),
                    raw_bytes.len(),
                    sanitized.as_mut_ptr(),
                    sanitized.len(),
                    &mut out_written,
                )
            };

            let final_bytes = if status == 0 && out_written > 0 {
                &sanitized[..out_written]
            } else {
                raw_bytes.as_slice()
            };

            use base64::Engine;
            let b64 = base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(final_bytes);
            Ok(format!("data:image/png;base64,{}", b64))
        } else {
            Err(format!("Não foi possível capturar o frame da tela do celular ({})", serial))
        }
    }

    /// Envia um toque na tela via `adb -s <serial> shell input tap x y`
    pub fn tap_screen(serial: &str, x: u32, y: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .args(["-s", serial, "shell", "input", "tap", &x.to_string(), &y.to_string()])
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao enviar toque ADB: {}", e))?;

        if output.status.success() {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err("Comando tap ADB falhou".to_string())
        }
    }

    /// Envia um evento de tecla via `adb -s <serial> shell input keyevent <keycode>`
    pub fn send_keyevent(serial: &str, keycode: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .args(["-s", serial, "shell", "input", "keyevent", &keycode.to_string()])
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao enviar keyevent ADB: {}", e))?;

        if output.status.success() {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err("Comando keyevent ADB falhou".to_string())
        }
    }

    /// Digitação humana tecla-por-tecla via C++ HPC Gaussian Delay (caractere a caractere)
    pub fn type_text_human_hpc(serial: &str, text: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        for ch in text.chars() {
            let char_delay_ms = Self::calculate_gaussian_delay(45, 160);
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(char_delay_ms as u64));

            let ch_str = match ch {
                ' ' => "%s".to_string(),
                '"' => "\\\"".to_string(),
                '\\' => "\\\\".to_string(),
                '&' => "\\&".to_string(),
                '<' => "\\<".to_string(),
                '>' => "\\>".to_string(),
                '|' => "\\|".to_string(),
                c => c.to_string(),
            };

            let _ = Command::new("adb")
                .args(["-s", serial, "shell", "input", "text", &ch_str])
                .output();
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn get_device_info(serial: &str) -> (String, String) {
        let brand_out = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("getprop")
            .arg("ro.product.brand")
            .output();
        
        let model_out = Command::new("adb")
            .arg("-s")
            .arg(serial)
            .arg("shell")
            .arg("getprop")
            .arg("ro.product.model")
            .output();

        let brand = match brand_out {
            Ok(o) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string(),
            Err(_) => "Android".to_string(),
        };

        let model = match model_out {
            Ok(o) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string(),
            Err(_) => "Generic Device".to_string(),
        };

        (brand, model)
    }

    /// Lança o Scrcpy H.264 nativo em 60 FPS com aceleração por hardware
    pub fn launch_scrcpy(serial: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let title = format!("Esfera v3 — Stream H.264 ({})", serial);
        let result = Command::new("scrcpy")
            .args([
                "-s",
                serial,
                "--max-size",
                "1080",
                "--max-fps",
                "60",
                "--video-bit-rate",
                "4M",
                "--window-title",
                &title,
            ])
            .spawn();

        match result {
            Ok(_) => Ok(format!("Scrcpy H.264 inicializado com sucesso para {}!", serial)),
            Err(e) => Err(format!(
                "Scrcpy nativo não encontrado no sistema ({}). Certifique-se de que o executável 'scrcpy.exe' esteja instalado e adicionado ao PATH do Windows.",
                e
            )),
        }
    }

    /// Lança o fluxo de vídeo da Câmera (Traseira ou Frontal) do celular Android via Scrcpy
    pub fn launch_scrcpy_camera(serial: &str, facing: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let camera_arg = if facing == "front" { "front" } else { "back" };
        let title = format!("Esfera v3 — Câmera Ao Vivo ({}) [{}]", serial, camera_arg.to_uppercase());

        let result = Command::new("scrcpy")
            .args([
                "-s",
                serial,
                "--video-source=camera",
                &format!("--camera-facing={}", camera_arg),
                "--max-fps",
                "60",
                "--window-title",
                &title,
            ])
            .spawn();

        match result {
            Ok(_) => Ok(format!("Transmissão de Câmera ({}) iniciada com sucesso para {}!", camera_arg, serial)),
            Err(e) => {
                let fallback = Command::new("scrcpy")
                    .args(["-s", serial, "--window-title", &title])
                    .spawn();
                match fallback {
                    Ok(_) => Ok(format!("Espelhamento de tela ativado para {}!", serial)),
                    Err(_) => Err(format!("Falha ao iniciar transmissão de câmera: {}. Verifique se o scrcpy.exe está instalado.", e)),
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Injeta a imagem da tela do computador ou QR Code diretamente na Câmera Virtual do Celular
    pub fn inject_virtual_camera_feed(serial: &str, target_type: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        // Passo 1: Executa a varredura ADB de pacotes de Câmera Virtual instalados no Android
        let pm_out = Command::new("adb")
            .args(["-s", serial, "shell", "pm", "list", "packages"])
            .output();

        let packages = match pm_out {
            Ok(o) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).to_string(),
            Err(_) => "".to_string(),
        };

        // Passo 2: Se o app de Câmera Virtual não estiver instalado, faz a instalação automatizada do APK Agente
        if !packages.contains("com.esfera.virtualcamera") && !packages.contains("com.vcam") {
            let apk_path = "public/Esfera_Agent_ADB.apk";
            let install_out = Command::new("adb")
                .args(["-s", serial, "install", "-r", apk_path])
                .output();

            if let Err(e) = install_out {
                return Err(format!("Falha ao injetar módulo de Câmera Virtual no Android: {}", e));
            }
        }

        // Passo 3: Envia Intent de broadcast via ADB IPC para substituir a alimentação de vídeo
        let intent_action = if target_type == "screen" {
            "com.esfera.virtualcamera.INJECT_SCREEN"
        } else {
            "com.esfera.virtualcamera.INJECT_QRCODE"
        };

        let broadcast_out = Command::new("adb")
            .args([
                "-s",
                serial,
                "shell",
                "am",
                "broadcast",
                "-a",
                intent_action,
                "--es",
                "source",
                "desktop_pc_v3",
            ])
            .output();

        match broadcast_out {
            Ok(o) => {
                let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout);
                Ok(format!(
                    "🎯 Injeção de Câmera Virtual ({}) ativada com sucesso no celular {}!\nIPC Status: {}",
                    target_type, serial, stdout.trim()
                ))
            }
            Err(e) => Err(format!("Falha ao enviar sinal de Câmera Virtual via ADB IPC: {}", e)),
        }
    }

    /// Executa o pacote completo de otimização de alta performance (HPC Turbo Tuning) no celular via ADB
    pub fn optimize_device_performance(serial: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let commands = [
            // 1. Desativa animações do Android para resposta instantânea ao toque (0.0x)
            vec!["shell", "settings", "put", "global", "window_animation_scale", "0.0"],
            vec!["shell", "settings", "put", "global", "transition_animation_scale", "0.0"],
            vec!["shell", "settings", "put", "global", "animator_duration_scale", "0.0"],
            // 2. Força renderização por GPU e aceleração por Hardware
            vec!["shell", "setprop", "debug.sf.hw", "1"],
            vec!["shell", "settings", "put", "global", "force_gpu_rendering", "1"],
            // 3. Imuniza o WhatsApp no Doze Mode para evitar que o Android mate em segundo plano
            vec!["shell", "dumpsys", "deviceidle", "whitelist", "+com.whatsapp"],
            vec!["shell", "dumpsys", "deviceidle", "whitelist", "+com.whatsapp.w4b"],
            // 4. Libera memória RAM e otimiza compilação DEX AOT (Speed Profile)
            vec!["shell", "cmd", "package", "compile", "-m", "speed-profile", "com.whatsapp"],
        ];

        let mut success_count = 0;
        for cmd in &commands {
            let res = Command::new("adb")
                .arg("-s")
                .arg(serial)
                .args(cmd)
                .output();
            if let Ok(o) = res {
                if o.status.success() {
                    success_count += 1;
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(format!(
            "🚀 Otimização HPC aplicada com sucesso em {}! ({}/{} otimizações ativadas)",
            serial, success_count, commands.len()
        ))
    }



    /// Instala silenciosamente o aplicativo Agente Esfera v3 (.APK) no celular via ADB e ativa as permissões de segundo plano
    pub fn install_agent_apk(serial: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let apk_path = if std::path::Path::new("Esfera_Agent_ADB.apk").exists() {
            "Esfera_Agent_ADB.apk"
        } else {
            "public/Esfera_Agent_ADB.apk"
        };

        let output = Command::new("adb")
            .args(["-s", serial, "install", "-r", "-g", apk_path])
            .output()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao executar 'adb install': {}", e))?;

        if output.status.success() {
            let _ = Command::new("adb")
                .args(["-s", serial, "shell", "am", "start", "-n", "com.mouldybread.adbautoenable/.MainActivity"])
                .output();

            let _ = Self::deploy_keepalive(serial);

            Ok(format!("📱 App Agente Esfera v3 instalado e ativado no celular {} com sucesso!", serial))
        } else {
            let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
            Err(format!("Falha na instalação do APK no celular: {}", stderr.trim()))
        }
    }

    /// Inicializa a Thread de Reconexão Automática Contínua para que NENHUM celular seja perdido após reinício do servidor
    pub fn start_auto_reconnect_daemon(db_conn: std::sync::Arc<std::sync::Mutex<crate::db::Database>>) {
        std::thread::spawn(move || loop {
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(12));
            if let Ok(db) = db_conn.lock() {
                let devices = db.get_devices(1);
                for dev in devices {
                    if dev.serial.contains(':') {
                        let _ = Command::new("adb")
                            .arg("connect")
                            .arg(&dev.serial)
                            .output();
                    } else if !dev.ip.is_empty() {
                        let target = format!("{}:{}", dev.ip, dev.port);
                        let _ = Command::new("adb")
                            .arg("connect")
                            .arg(&target)
                            .output();
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }
}


// ========================================================
// ARQUIVO: src-tauri\src\ai_engine.rs
// ========================================================

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Motor de Inteligência Artificial (ai_engine.rs)
// Suporte nativo a Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic e Custom Endpoint
// ============================================================

use crate::db::AiConfig;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;
use std::time::Duration;

pub struct AiEngine;

impl AiEngine {
    /// Reescreve ou gera mensagens personalizadas utilizando o provedor configurado
    pub async fn generate_message(
        config: &AiConfig,
        base_message: &str,
        contact_name: &str,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        let client = Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(15))
            .build()
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha ao criar cliente HTTP IA: {}", e))?;

        let prompt = if !config.system_prompt.is_empty() {
            format!(
                "{}\n\nReescreva a mensagem para o contato '{}':\n{}",
                config.system_prompt, contact_name, base_message
            )
        } else {
            format!(
                "Reescreva a seguinte mensagem comercial para o contato '{}' de forma natural, amigável e persuasiva, mantendo a variável {{nome}} se houver:\n{}",
                contact_name, base_message
            )
        };

        let raw_response = match config.provider.to_lowercase().as_str() {
            "ollama" => Self::call_ollama(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            "openai" => Self::call_openai(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            "anthropic" => Self::call_anthropic(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            "custom" => Self::call_custom_endpoint(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
            _ => Self::call_ollama(&client, config, &prompt).await?,
        };

        let sanitized = Self::sanitize_ai_response(&raw_response, contact_name);
        Ok(sanitized)
    }

    /// Requisição para Ollama Local/Cloud (API /api/generate)
    async fn call_ollama(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let endpoint = if config.endpoint.is_empty() {
            "http://localhost:11434/api/generate".to_string()
        } else if !config.endpoint.contains("/api/generate") {
            format!("{}/api/generate", config.endpoint.trim_end_matches('/'))
        } else {
            config.endpoint.clone()
        };

        let model = if config.model.is_empty() { "llama3".to_string() } else { config.model.clone() };

        let payload = json!({
            "model": model,
            "prompt": prompt,
            "stream": false,
            "options": {
                "temperature": config.temperature
            }
        });

        let res = client.post(&endpoint)
            .json(&payload)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha na chamada ao Ollama: {}", e))?;

        if !res.status().is_success() {
            return Err(format!("Ollama retornou status de erro HTTP: {}", res.status()));
        }

        let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await.map_err(|e| format!("Resposta Ollama inválida: {}", e))?;
        body["response"].as_str().map(|s| s.to_string()).ok_or_else(|| "Resposta Ollama vazia".to_string())
    }

    /// Requisição para OpenAI API (/v1/chat/completions)
    async fn call_openai(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let endpoint = if config.endpoint.is_empty() {
            "https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions".to_string()
        } else if !config.endpoint.contains("/chat/completions") {
            format!("{}/v1/chat/completions", config.endpoint.trim_end_matches('/'))
        } else {
            config.endpoint.clone()
        };

        let model = if config.model.is_empty() { "gpt-4o-mini".to_string() } else { config.model.clone() };

        let payload = json!({
            "model": model,
            "temperature": config.temperature,
            "messages": [
                { "role": "system", "content": "Você é um assistente especialista em redação de mensagens para WhatsApp." },
                { "role": "user", "content": prompt }
            ]
        });

        let req = client.post(&endpoint)
            .json(&payload);

        let req = if !config.api_key.is_empty() {
            req.bearer_auth(&config.api_key)
        } else {
            req
        };

        let res = req.send().await.map_err(|e| format!("Falha na chamada OpenAI: {}", e))?;
        if !res.status().is_success() {
            return Err(format!("OpenAI retornou erro HTTP: {}", res.status()));
        }

        let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await.map_err(|e| format!("Resposta OpenAI inválida: {}", e))?;
        body["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
            .as_str()
            .map(|s| s.to_string())
            .ok_or_else(|| "Conteúdo OpenAI não encontrado".to_string())
    }

    /// Requisição para Anthropic API (/v1/messages)
    async fn call_anthropic(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let endpoint = if config.endpoint.is_empty() {
            "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages".to_string()
        } else {
            config.endpoint.clone()
        };

        let model = if config.model.is_empty() { "claude-3-haiku-20240307".to_string() } else { config.model.clone() };

        let payload = json!({
            "model": model,
            "max_tokens": 1000,
            "messages": [
                { "role": "user", "content": prompt }
            ]
        });

        let res = client.post(&endpoint)
            .header("x-api-key", &config.api_key)
            .header("anthropic-version", "2023-06-01")
            .json(&payload)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Falha na chamada Anthropic: {}", e))?;

        if !res.status().is_success() {
            return Err(format!("Anthropic retornou erro HTTP: {}", res.status()));
        }

        let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await.map_err(|e| format!("Resposta Anthropic inválida: {}", e))?;
        body["content"][0]["text"]
            .as_str()
            .map(|s| s.to_string())
            .ok_or_else(|| "Conteúdo Anthropic não encontrado".to_string())
    }

    /// Requisição para Endpoint Customizado (formato OpenAI)
    async fn call_custom_endpoint(client: &Client, config: &AiConfig, prompt: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        Self::call_openai(client, config, prompt).await
    }

    /// Sanitiza o texto retornado pela IA para remover markdown, aspas e ajustar o nome
    pub fn sanitize_ai_response(raw_text: &str, contact_name: &str) -> String {
        let mut text = raw_text.trim().to_string();

        if text.starts_with('"') && text.ends_with('"') && text.len() > 2 {
            text = text[1..text.len() - 1].to_string();
        }

        text = text.replace("{nome}", contact_name);
        text
    }
}

// ============================================================
// DISPOSITIVOS
// ============================================================

use std::sync::RwLock;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::time::Instant;

static BATTERY_CACHE: std::sync::LazyLock<RwLock<HashMap<String, (i32, Instant)>>> =
    std::sync::LazyLock::new(|| RwLock::new(HashMap::new()));

fn get_cached_battery(serial: &str) -> i32 {
    let now = Instant::now();
    if let Ok(cache) = BATTERY_CACHE.read() {
        if let Some((level, timestamp)) = cache.get(serial) {
            if now.duration_since(*timestamp).as_secs() < 60 {
                return *level;
            }
        }
    }

    let real_bat = AdbManager::get_battery_level(serial).unwrap_or(85);
    if let Ok(mut cache) = BATTERY_CACHE.write() {
        cache.insert(serial.to_string(), (real_bat, now));
    }
    real_bat
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn get_devices(state: State<'_, AppState>, company_id: i32) -> Result<Vec<Device>, String> {
    let target_company_id = if company_id <= 0 { 1 } else { company_id };
    let db = state.db.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut db_devices = db.get_devices(target_company_id);
    let active_adb_list = AdbManager::list_devices().unwrap_or_default();

    for adb_dev in &active_adb_list {
        if adb_dev.serial == "*" || adb_dev.serial.starts_with('*') {
            continue;
        }

        let is_wireless = adb_dev.serial.contains(':') || adb_dev.serial.contains('.');

        let status = if adb_dev.state == "device" {
            "online".to_string()
        } else if adb_dev.state == "unauthorized" {
            "connecting".to_string()
        } else {
            "offline".to_string()
        };

        // Procura por dispositivo existente no DB por serial exato
        let existing_idx = db_devices.iter().position(|d| d.serial == adb_dev.serial);

        if let Some(idx) = existing_idx {
            // Atualiza status e nivel de bateria com cache ultrarrapido (RAM 0ms)
            db_devices[idx].status = status;
            if adb_dev.state == "device" {
                db_devices[idx].battery = get_cached_battery(&adb_dev.serial);
            }
            db.upsert_device(&db_devices[idx])?;
        } else {
            // Dispositivo novo: lê dados de marca/modelo e bateria apenas UMA vez
            let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&adb_dev.serial);
            let battery = if adb_dev.state == "device" {
                get_cached_battery(&adb_dev.serial)
            } else {
                0
            };

            let new_dev = Device {
                id: 0,
                serial: adb_dev.serial.clone(),
                name: format!("Aparelho {}", db_devices.len() + 1),
                brand,
                model,
                ip: if is_wireless {
                    adb_dev.serial.split(':').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()
                } else {
                    "".to_string()
                },
                port: 5555,
                status,
                battery,
                company_id: target_company_id,
                device_number: (db_devices.len() + 1) as i32,
                sector: "junior".to_string(),
            };
            db.upsert_device(&new_dev)?;
        }
    }

    // 2. Atualiza o status em tempo real de cada dispositivo salvo no DB
    let mut refreshed_db = db.get_devices(target_company_id);
    let mut online_models = std::collections::HashSet::new();

    for dev in &mut refreshed_db {
        let is_active = active_adb_list.iter().any(|adb_d| adb_d.serial == dev.serial);
        if is_active {
            dev.status = "online".to_string();
            online_models.insert(dev.model.clone());
        } else {
            dev.status = "offline".to_string();
        }
    }

    // 3. Purga registros USB desatualizados offline se o mesmo modelo de celular já estiver online via Wi-Fi
    refreshed_db.retain(|dev| {
        if dev.status == "offline" && !dev.serial.contains(':') && online_models.contains(&dev.model) {
            let _ = db.delete_device(&dev.serial);
            false
        } else {
            true
        }
    });

    Ok(refreshed_db)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn connect_adb_device(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    ip: String,
    port: i32,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    // Se o usuário digitou/colou "192.168.1.82:9093", extrai o IP e a Porta automaticamente!
    let (target_ip, target_port) = if ip.contains(':') {
        let parts: Vec<&str> = ip.split(':').collect();
        let parsed_port = parts.get(1).and_then(|p| p.trim().parse::<i32>().ok()).unwrap_or(if port <= 0 { 5555 } else { port });
        (parts[0].trim().to_string(), parsed_port)
    } else {
        (ip.trim().to_string(), if port <= 0 { 5555 } else { port })
    };

    let result = AdbManager::connect_device(&target_ip, target_port)?;
    let db = state.db.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let serial = format!("{}:{}", target_ip, target_port);
    let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&serial);
    let final_name = device_name
        .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));
    let dev = Device {
        id: 0,
        serial: serial.clone(),
        name: final_name,
        brand,
        model,
        ip: target_ip,
        port: target_port,
        status: "online".to_string(),
        battery: 90,
        company_id,
        device_number,
        sector,
    };
    db.upsert_device(&dev)?;
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&serial);
    let _ = db.add_log(
        company_id,
        "info",
        "ADB Engine",
        &format!("Dispositivo {} conectado via wireless na porta {}.", serial, target_port),
    );
    Ok(result)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn pair_and_connect_adb_device(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    ip: String,
    pair_port: u16,
    pair_code: String,
    connect_port: u16,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let clean_ip = ip.split(':').next().unwrap_or(&ip).trim().to_string();
    let pair_res = AdbManager::pair_device(&clean_ip, pair_port, &pair_code)?;

    let conn_port = if connect_port == 0 { 5555 } else { connect_port };
    let connect_res = AdbManager::connect_device(&clean_ip, conn_port as i32)?;

    let wireless_serial = format!("{}:{}", clean_ip, conn_port);
    let _ = std::process::Command::new("adb")
        .args(["-s", &wireless_serial, "shell", "setprop", "persist.adb.tcp.port", "5555"])
        .output();
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&wireless_serial);

    let db = state.db.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&wireless_serial);
    let final_name = device_name
        .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));

    let device = Device {
        id: 0,
        serial: wireless_serial,
        name: final_name,
        brand,
        model,
        ip: clean_ip,
        port: conn_port as i32,
        status: "online".to_string(),
        battery: 100,
        company_id,
        device_number,
        sector,
    };
    db.upsert_device(&device)?;

    Ok(format!("🔥 Celular Pareado e Conectado com Sucesso! {} ({})", pair_res, connect_res))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn disconnect_adb_device(state: State<'_, AppState>, serial: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    let result = AdbManager::disconnect_device(&serial)?;
    let db = state.db.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let _ = db.add_log(
        0,
        "info",
        "ADB Engine",
        &format!("Dispositivo {} desconectado.", serial),
    );
    Ok(result)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn optimize_device(serial: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    let commands = vec![
        "dumpsys deviceidle disable",
        "settings put global wifi_sleep_policy 2",
        "settings put global low_power 0",
        "settings put global low_power_trigger_level 0",
        "settings put global wifi_scan_always_enabled 1",
        "settings put global stay_on_while_plugged_in 7",
        "settings put global adb_allowed_connection_time 0",
        "setprop persist.adb.tcp.port 5555",
        "dumpsys deviceidle whitelist +com.google.android.gms",
        "dumpsys deviceidle whitelist +com.android.shell",
    ];
    let mut results = Vec::new();
    for cmd in commands {
        let output = std::process::Command::new("adb")
            .args(["-s", &serial, "shell", cmd])
            .output();
        match output {
            Ok(_) => results.push(format!("✓ {}", cmd)),
            Err(e) => results.push(format!("✗ {}: {}", cmd, e)),
        }
    }
    Ok(results.join("\n"))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn set_chip_restriction(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    chip_id: i32,
    restriction_type: String,
    days: Option<i32>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let db = state.db.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let restricted_until = if restriction_type == "restrito_custom" {
        let d = days.unwrap_or(1).max(1);
        format!("{} days", d)
    } else if restriction_type == "restrito_7d" {
        "7 days".to_string()
    } else {
        "1 day".to_string()
    };
    db.set_chip_restriction(chip_id, &restriction_type, &restricted_until)
}

// ============================================================

// ============================================================

#[tauri::command]
pub fn list_usb_devices() -> Result<Vec<AdbDeviceInfo>, String> {
    AdbManager::list_devices()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn link_usb_to_wireless(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    serial: String,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let ip = AdbManager::get_device_wifi_ip(&serial)?;
    AdbManager::enable_tcpip(&serial, 5555)?;
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(1500));
    let link_msg = AdbManager::connect_device(&ip, 5555)?;
    let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&serial);
    let db = state.db.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let wireless_serial = format!("{}:5555", ip);
    let final_name = device_name
        .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));
    let device = Device {
        id: 0,
        serial: wireless_serial.clone(),
        name: final_name,
        brand,
        model,
        ip,
        port: 5555,
        status: "online".to_string(),
        battery: 100,
        company_id,
        device_number,
        sector,
    };
    db.upsert_device(&device)?;
    // Injeta regras Keep-Alive no celular
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&wireless_serial);
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&serial);
    Ok(format!(
        "Sucesso! Dispositivo pareado sem fio: {}",
        link_msg
    ))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn force_enable_wireless_adb(
    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    serial: String,
    company_id: i32,
    device_number: i32,
    sector: String,
    device_name: Option<String>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let ip = AdbManager::get_device_wifi_ip(&serial).map_err(|e| {
        format!("{} Certifique-se de que o celular está conectado na mesma rede Wi-Fi que o computador.", e)
    })?;

    let _ = std::process::Command::new("adb")
        .args(["-s", &serial, "shell", "setprop", "persist.adb.tcp.port", "5555"])
        .output();
    AdbManager::enable_tcpip(&serial, 5555)?;
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(300));

    let connect_res = AdbManager::connect_device(&ip, 5555)?;
    let (brand, model) = AdbManager::get_device_info(&serial);
    let wireless_serial = format!("{}:5555", ip);

    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&wireless_serial);
    let _ = AdbManager::deploy_keepalive(&serial);

    let db = state.db.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let final_name = device_name
        .filter(|n| !n.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("Aparelho {}", device_number));

    let device = Device {
        id: 0,
        serial: wireless_serial,
        name: final_name,
        brand,
        model,
        ip: ip.clone(),
        port: 5555,
        status: "online".to_string(),
        battery: 100,
        company_id,
        device_number,
        sector,
    };
    db.upsert_device(&device)?;

    Ok(format!(
        "🔥 Sucesso! Depuração Wi-Fi forçada e ativada no celular com IP {}! ({})",
        ip, connect_res
    ))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn rewrite_message_ai(message: String, contact_name: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    // Mock AI rewrite - adds temporal greeting and name personalization
    use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
    let seed = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap()
        .as_millis() as usize;

    let hour = {
        // Rough UTC-3 hour for Brazil
        let h = (seed / 3600000) % 24;
        if h < 12 {
            "Bom dia"
        } else if h < 18 {
            "Boa tarde"
        } else {
            "Boa noite"
        }
    };

    let greetings = ["Olá", "Oi", "Fala", "E aí"];
    let closings = ["Abraços", "Att", "Equipe REALESS", "Setor Financeiro"];
    let g = greetings[seed % greetings.len()];
    let c = closings[(seed / 7) % closings.len()];

    let personalized = message.replace("{nome}", &contact_name);
    Ok(format!("{}, {}! {}\n\n{}", hour, g, personalized, c))
}

// ============================================================
// Esfera v3 — Camada de Banco de Dados (SQLite Local)
// Mapeamento 1:1 das tabelas ESF_* do Esfera v2
// Fase 1: WAL, Soft Delete, 4 tabelas novas, Hash SHA-256
// ============================================================

use rusqlite::{params, Connection, Result, Transaction};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Sha256, Digest};
